Motivation: 自主和代理型AI的使用使监管对象的知识和控制权不再局限于传统监管对象，而是分散在AI供应链中，导致现有监管假设失效，需要新的监管方法。

Method: 通过分析四个监管系统（英国内容平台监管、数据保护、英国金融服务、欧盟AI法案跨部门制度），研究自主和代理型AI带来的挑战并提出潜在解决方案。

Result: 现有监管模型无法有效应对AI自主性，事后监督作为风险管理工具失效，AI自主性产生新的系统性风险。

Conclusion: 监管需要从被动反应转变为主动过程，将AI供应链纳入监管范围，采用全新治理方法来适应AI自主性的挑战。

Abstract: Regulating activities where regulatees use autonomous and agentic AI is challenging. Regulatory assumptions about regulatee knowledge and control no longer hold true; much of that lies elsewhere in the AI supply chain which thus needs to be brought within the scope of regulation. Governance systems for autonomous AI cannot replicate existing governance models, but need a fresh approach. Retrospective supervisory oversight becomes ineffective as a risk management tool, and AI autonomy generates new systemic risks which require new solutions. This paper investigate four regulatory systems: UK regulation of content platforms, data protection, UK financial services, and the EU AI Act\'92s cross-sectoral regime. It analyses the challenges posed by autonomous and agentic AI and proposes potential solutions which regulators might adopt. These will transform regulation from a reactive process to an active one, and assist it in adapting to the challenges of AI autonomy.

</details>


### [182] [The Boundaries of Automation: A Theory of Persistent Human Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21547)
*Fares Fourati,Hinrich Schütze,Eyke Hüllermeier,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“人类参与AI系统只是因为AI不够强大”的假设，提出即使AI高度发达，人类参与仍会因三种理由（技术互补、规范发展、目标涌现）而持续存在，人机共建不是权宜之计，而是具有涌现性目标活动的持久特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI研究隐含地假设人类参与只是暂时的——因为AI还不够好。本文质疑这一假设，试图探讨自动化的概念性局限，论证即便AI系统高度强大，人类参与仍然有其必要性。

Method: 通过理论分析，区分了人类参与持续存在的三种理由：技术/互补性理由（人类提供AI缺少的能力或视角）、规范/发展理由（参与本身对人类的能动性或学习有价值）、涌现理由（活动目标本身在交互过程中才得以生成和确定）。

Result: 提出人类-AI共建（co-construction）不是对不完美AI的临时反应，而是那些目标通过参与本身涌现出来的活动的持久特征。目标涌现（target emergence）是关键概念：当活动目标无法预先完整指定，而是在交互中生成时，人类参与就构成目标本身的一部分。

Conclusion: 该视角对自动化的局限性、未来AI系统的设计、评估和伦理具有重要启示：自动化有概念性边界，人机交互不是过渡阶段而是持久范式。

Abstract: The rapid progress of AI has intensified the long-standing pursuit of automation: replacing human participation with algorithms wherever possible. Implicit in this pursuit is the assumption that humans remain in the loop only because current AI systems are not yet sufficiently capable. This paper challenges that assumption. Rather than asking how far automation can extend, we ask where its conceptual limits lie and argue that human participation may persist even with highly capable AI systems for three distinct reasons. Technical or complementarity grounds arise when humans contribute capabilities or perspectives unavailable to AI. Normative or developmental grounds arise when participation itself is valuable for human agency or learning. Most importantly, emergence grounds arise from target emergence: in some activities, the target is not fully specified in advance but instead emerges through the interaction itself. In these cases, human participation is not merely a means of improving execution but is constitutive of the target being produced. Human--AI co-construction, understood as the joint production of outcomes by humans and AI systems, is therefore not simply a temporary response to imperfect AI, but a persistent feature of activities whose objectives emerge through participation. This perspective has important implications for the limits of automation and for the design, evaluation, and ethics of future AI systems.

</details>


### [183] [SPORD: A Simulation-Propose-then-OR-Dispose Approach for Supply Chain Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21354)
*Jiayin He,Yutong Pan,Sen Yang,Ningxuan Kang,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPORD方法（Simulation-Propose-then-OR-Dispose），通过解耦模拟与优化，解决电商供应链规划中模型碎片化、计算复杂性和执行信任三大障碍。该方法已部署为京东NetSim平台，实现跨区域履约率从6.1%降至4.9%，月均碳减排约5,745 tCO2e。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商供应链规划长期以孤岛项目形式运作，面临三大障碍：运营碎片化（模型标准化困难）、计算棘手性（SKU/节点/路由组合规模超出求解器处理能力）和实施障碍（最优解因缺乏信任而无法落地）。

Method: 提出SPORD方法，核心思想是解耦：模拟阶段生成并评估所有运营上可行的候选路径，吸收所有特定业务逻辑；整数规划阶段从中选择全局最优子集。采用矩阵向量化CPU/GPU加速模拟（比串行方法快10-100倍），以及列表调度算法将耦合订单处理从数小时缩短至分钟级，并通过智能诊断引擎建立闭环。

Result: 自2025年部署以来，NetSim已为超过20,000家供应商优化端到端服务，跨区域履约率从6.1%降至4.9%，月均碳减排约5,745 tCO2e。

Conclusion: SPORD将模拟从监控角色转向主动规划，透明输出将怀疑的高管转变为积极参与的合作者，模块化架构确保下次规划仅需配置而非重建。

Abstract: For years, supply chain planning at e-commerce firms has operated as a collection of isolated projects. Each planning task from static network planning to dynamic warehouse assortment planning requires analysts to spend weeks building models from scratch, calibrating and persuading executives to act on outputs they cannot verify. Three barriers drive this: bespoke models proliferate because standardization is difficult (operational fragmentation); once unified, the combinatorial scale of millions of SKUs, thousands of nodes, and intricate routing logic exceeds what solvers can handle within a tight window (computational intractability); and a mathematically optimal solution still fails to be implemented if the executives do not trust it (implementation hurdle). To bridge this gap, we propose and implement the Simulation-Propose-then-OR-Dispose method, deployed as JD.com's NetSim platform. The central insight is decoupling: simulation proposes by generating and evaluating the full set of operationally valid candidate paths-absorbing all idiosyncratic business logic, while an integer program disposes by selecting the globally optimal subset. Computationally, matrix-vectorized CPU/GPU accelerated simulation achieves a 10-100 times speedup over serial methods, and a list scheduling algorithm reduces coupled-order processing from hours to minutes. Operationally, we establish a closed loop via an intelligent diagnosis engine. Since 2025, NetSim has optimized end to-end services for over 20,000 suppliers, the cross-regional fulfillment rate dropped from 6.1% to 4.9%, and the average monthly carbon reduction is approximately 5,745 tCO2e. SPORD moves simulation from monitoring to active planning. The transparent outputs turn skeptical executives into engaged collaborators, and the modular architecture ensures that the next planning requires just configuration, not reconstruction.

</details>


### [184] [OpenForgeRL: Train Harness-native Agents in Any Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21557)
*Xiao Yu,Baolin Peng,Ruize Xu,Hao Zou,Qianhui Wu,Hao Cheng,Wenlin Yao,Nikhil Singh,Zhou Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenForgeRL 是一个开源框架，用于对基于推理框架（harness）的AI代理进行端到端训练。它通过代理服务模型调用并记录训练数据，结合Kubernetes编排器实现大规模分布式训练，可兼容多种框架和环境。实验表明，该框架在Claw、GUI浏览器和计算机操作等多种代理任务上显著优于同类基线模型，并分析了不同框架和RL对代理行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI代理依赖复杂的推理框架（如Claude Code、Codex、OpenClaw）进行多轮推理、工具使用和外部系统访问，但这些框架端到端训练困难，因为开放基础设施的SFT/RL栈无法原生表达有状态、多进程的框架推理过程。

Method: 提出OpenForgeRL框架，核心包括：（1）轻量级代理服务，接管框架的模型调用并将其记录为训练数据；（2）Kubernetes编排器，每个rollout在独立的远程容器中运行；（3）解耦训练与推理，使得任何框架和环境的代理都可以端到端训练。

Result: 在ClawEval上OpenForgeClaw达到31.7 pass^3和55.9 pass@3，QwenClawBench上33.7；在GUI任务中，OpenForgeGUI在OSWorld-Verified上37.7，Online-Mind2Web上63.0，WebVoyager上72.3。在几乎所有基准测试上超越同规模开源基线，GUI任务中甚至超越数倍大的模型。分析发现某些框架更难学习，RL能提升代理的自验证、工具覆盖率和多步计划完成能力，但错误恢复能力仍然薄弱。

Conclusion: OpenForgeRL有效解决了基于框架的AI代理端到端训练难题，能够在实际部署的框架和环境中直接训练代理。不同推理框架对学习难度有显著影响，RL训练能提升代理可靠性但错误恢复能力仍有待改进。

Abstract: Modern AI agents rely on elaborate inference harnesses such as Claude Code, Codex, and OpenClaw to drive multi-turn reasoning, tool use, and access to external systems. While powerful, these complex harnesses also make agents hard to train end-to-end with open infrastructure, whose SFT/RL stacks cannot natively express stateful, multi-process harness inference. To address this, we present OpenForgeRL, an open-source framework for training harness-based agents end-to-end in diverse environments. OpenForgeRL achieves this with a lightweight proxy that serves the harness's model calls while recording them as training data for a standard RL codebase (e.g., veRL), and a Kubernetes orchestrator that runs each rollout in its own remote container, together enabling training on any harness in any environment at scale. By decoupling training and inference, OpenForgeRL allows researchers to easily train, study, and improve agents directly in the real harnesses and environments they are deployed with. We validate our framework across diverse, complex harnesses and environments, spanning tool/claw-based agents and multimodal GUI browser- and computer-use agents. Using only hundreds to a few thousand tasks, OpenForgeClaw reaches 31.7 pass^3 and 55.9 pass@3 on ClawEval and 33.7 on QwenClawBench. OpenForgeGUI reaches 37.7 on OSWorld-Verified, 63.0 on Online-Mind2Web, and 72.3 on WebVoyager. Both outperform open baselines of similar size on nearly all benchmarks, and in the GUI setting match or surpass models several times larger. Beyond benchmarks, we analyze how harness choice (e.g., ZeroClaw, OpenClaw, Codex) and RL shape agent behavior. We find that some harnesses are substantially harder to learn than others, and that RL improves agentic reliability, such as self-verification, tool coverage, and completing multi-step plans, though critical abilities such as error recovery remain weak.

</details>


### [185] [Towards Faithful Graph Explanations with Synergistic Edge Effects via Granular Balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21381)
*Jiancu Chen,Shuyin Xia,Guan Wang,Degang Chen,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeeExplainer是一种无参数的可解释性方法，通过粒度球图细化机制捕捉边之间的协同效应，并在结构图上扰动节点和边来生成解释子图，在多个图分类数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN解释方法在评估边重要性时往往忽略边之间的协同效应，导致对边重要性的刻画不够准确。

Method: 提出SeeExplainer：1) 引入粒度球图细化机制，将图分解为若干无固定大小的粒度球，并以此作为节点构建结构图，从而捕捉边之间的协同效应；2) 在结构图上扰动节点和边，基于各自的贡献生成解释子图。该方法无需参数。

Result: 在多个不同网络结构的图分类数据集上，SeeExplainer的性能优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 考虑边之间的协同效应能够更准确地生成解释子图，无参数的设计使得该方法具有较好的通用性。

Abstract: Instance-level explanations aim to reveal the rationale behind a model's decisions for a specific graph. Previous methods explain graph neural networks (GNNs) by selecting important edges to induce subgraphs, where edge importance is assessed by perturbing each edge and observing changes in the model predictions. However, they often neglect the synergistic effects among edges, which are crucial for accurately characterizing edge importance. To address this issue, we propose SeeExplainer, a parameter-free explainer to interpret GNNs. Specifically, we first introduce a granular-ball graph refinement mechanism that decomposes a graph into several disjoint granular-balls with no fixed size, and utilize them as nodes to construct a structural graph. This process can better capture the synergistic effects among edges. Then, we perturb nodes and edges in the structural graph to generate explanatory subgraphs based on their respective contributions. Experiments on several graph classification datasets of different networks show that SeeExplainer outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [186] [Multimodal Pretraining for Generalizable EEG Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21384)
*Targol Bakhtiarvand,Jugal Kalita,Adham Atyabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态EEG基础模型，结合Mamba架构的原始信号编码器、ViT风格的时间-频率编码器和轻量级文本编码器，通过掩码建模、跨视图对比对齐和时间一致性损失进行预训练，在CHB-MIT癫痫检测基准上达到SOTA性能（AUROC 0.874-0.878），并在留一法交叉验证中展现了患者独立的癫痫检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EEG模型通常局限于特定数据集和任务，跨数据集泛化能力差，难以在不同场景下应用。作者希望开发一个可适应多种EEG任务的基础模型，解决模型泛化性不足的问题。

Method: 构建多模态EEG基础模型，包含三个编码器：基于Mamba架构的原始信号编码器、ViT风格的时间-频率数据编码器、轻量级文本编码器。预训练采用掩码建模、跨视图对比对齐和时间一致性损失三种无监督技术，无需标注数据即可学习癫痫相关表征。

Result: 在CHB-MIT标准分割上，单模型AUROC达0.874，集成模型达0.878，达到SOTA；在留一法交叉验证（LOSO）下，19名受试者平均平衡准确率为0.558，展示了患者独立检测的难度。

Conclusion: 该多模态基础模型在不同数据集和评估设置下实现了鲁棒的癫痫检测，能够轻松适应新的检测场景，并支持可解释的癫痫定位。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) models used for epilepsy are often limited to specific datasets and tasks. This limited approach can make it challenging to apply these models across different datasets or in various situations. However, recent studies in foundation models and self-supervised learning suggest that an adaptable EEG backbone could support a range of EEG related tasks. In this study, we have developed a multimodal EEG foundation model that combines a raw signal encoder based on the Mamba architecture, a Vision Transformer (ViT)-style encoder for time-frequency data, and a lightweight encoder for text, all within a shared embedding space. The pretraining process relies on several innovative techniques, such as masked modeling, cross-view contrastive alignment, and temporal consistency losses. These methods are designed to create rich, seizure-relevant representations without requiring labeled data. To assess the efficacy and generalization of our pretrained model, we fine-tuned it on the canonical CHB-MIT seizure detection benchmark and additional seizure detection datasets, and conducted extensive experiments comparing different model variants. On the standard CHB-MIT split, our best single model achieved an AUROC of 0.874, and an ensemble variant reached 0.878 AUROC, representing state-of-the-art performance on this benchmark. In addition to standard train-test splits, we evaluated performance under a leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocol, which is rarely reported in prior EEG seizure modeling work and highlights the difficulty of patient-independent seizure detection, with a mean LOSO balanced accuracy of 0.558 across 19 subjects. Across datasets and evaluation settings, our multimodal foundation model enabled robust seizure detection and straightforward adaptation to new seizure detection scenarios, while also supporting interpretable seizure localization.

</details>


### [187] [MSBraM: A Multi-scale Self-supervised Brain Foundation Model for Hierarchical EEG Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21402)
*Tao Zhou,Jing Han,Lingyu Shu,Zixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MSBraM提出了一种多尺度自监督脑电基础模型，通过两阶段预训练框架（多尺度神经分词器 + 课程多尺度掩码策略）学习EEG信号中的层次化表征，在2400+小时EEG数据上预训练后，在12个公共数据集、10个下游任务上表现优于现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督EEG基础模型难以捕捉EEG信号固有的多尺度时间结构（局部神经模式与长程依赖关系），限制了跨尺度表征学习及在不同下游任务上的泛化能力。

Method: MSBraM采用两阶段预训练框架：1）多尺度神经分词器通过向量量化重建将原始EEG信号离散为不同时间分辨率的语义编码；2）使用课程多尺度掩码策略预训练模型预测被掩码的编码，逐步从细粒度局部模式过渡到全局时间上下文。

Result: 在2400+小时EEG数据上预训练，在12个公开数据集、10个下游任务上评估，MSBraM显著优于现有SOTA预训练模型，展现出强大的泛化性和迁移能力。

Conclusion: 显式建模多尺度时间动态是构建有效EEG基础模型的关键因素。

Abstract: Self-supervised foundation models have recently shown strong potential for electroencephalogram (EEG)-based analysis. However, existing approaches struggle to capture the inherently multi-scale temporal structure of EEG signals, where local neural patterns and long-range dependencies jointly encode task-relevant information. This limitation hampers cross-scale representation learning and generalization across diverse downstream tasks. To address this challenge, we propose MSBraM, a Multi-Scale self-supervised Brain foundation Model designed to learn hierarchical EEG representations. MSBraM follows a two-stage pretraining framework. First, a multi-scale neural tokenizer discretizes raw EEG signals into semantic codes at different temporal resolutions via vector-quantized reconstruction. Second, the model is pretrained to predict masked codes using a curriculum multi-scale masking strategy, progressively integrating fine-grained local patterns with global temporal context. We pretrain MSBraM on over 2,400 hours of EEG data and evaluate it across 10 downstream tasks on 12 public datasets. Extensive experiments show that MSBraM achieves superior performance on other state-of-the-art pretrained models, demonstrating strong generalization and transferability. These results indicate that explicitly modeling multi-scale temporal dynamics is critical for effective EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [188] [Logical Regression for Planning with Axioms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21414)
*Connor Little,Christian Muise*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在包含公理的规划域中近似计算动作逻辑回归的方法，该方法将条件限制在局部状态，避免重新计算公理，生成最小局部状态。实验表明该方法在执行监控场景中可将变量数量减少多达70%，并在意外变化环境中实现超过50%的恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑回归在自动规划中有广泛应用（如鲁棒规划执行和非确定性规划的紧凑策略），但在包含公理的规划域中计算复杂度显著增加，需要一种高效的近似方法。

Method: 提出一种近似逻辑回归的方法，将条件限制为局部状态（partial states），生成最小局部状态，同时避免重新计算公理。将该方法嵌入执行监控（execution monitoring）框架中验证效果。

Result: 在多个领域中将执行监控考虑的变量数量减少多达70%；在意外变化的环境中，多个领域的恢复率超过50%。

Conclusion: 该近似逻辑回归方法能够显著泛化局部状态，有效减少执行监控的复杂度，同时保持足够的鲁棒性以应对环境中的意外变化。

Abstract: In automated planning, logical regression is an operation that returns the most general condition necessary for an action to achieve a particular formula. It has many applications, such as allowing for more robust plan execution and providing compact policies for non-deterministic planning. Although relatively simple to calculate in basic planning settings, logical regression becomes significantly more complex when additional factors, such as axioms, are present. We introduce a methodology for approximating the logical regression of an action in a domain that includes axioms; an approximation that limits conditions to partial states. Our method produces minimal partial states while avoiding the recalculation of axioms. To demonstrate the impact of our methods, we embed our form of regression in an execution monitoring context, a well-established setting that can benefit greatly from logical regression. Our results show that this form of regression can dramatically generalize partial states across multiple domains, reducing the number of variables considered for execution monitoring by up to 70%, and demonstrate that the resulting execution monitor is robust enough to recover frequently in an environment with unexpected changes: several domains recover over 50% of the time in our tests.

</details>


### [189] [PATS: Policy-Aware Training Scaffolding for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21419)
*Yipeng Shi,Zhipeng Ma,Yue Wang,Qitai Tan,Yang Li,Peng Chen,Zhengzhou Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以策略为中心的训练范式Pats，将技能重新定义为动态训练脚手架。通过将最新策略的rollout组转换为证据卡，并根据任务特定评估调整后续rollout的上下文，帮助弱策略完成复杂任务。在ALFWorld和WebShop上提升高达18.6%，在七个搜索增强QA基准上使用更少提示词的同时保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长周期LLM智能体强化学习中，弱策略经常重复相似的失败，产生无信息的轨迹，限制了有效的策略优化。现有以技能为中心的方法虽然通过优化、过滤或内化可复用技能来改善探索，但它们仍然以技能本身为中心，而非被设计为对演化策略的自适应训练时支持。

Method: 提出Pats框架，采用以策略为中心的训练范式，将技能重新定义为动态训练脚手架。具体地，将最新策略的rollout组转换为证据卡，并使用任务特定评估来调整后续rollout中使用的上下文。为弱策略提供具体指导以完成挑战任务；随着策略提升，冗余上下文被修正或移除以减少对显式指导的依赖；策略使用标准RLVR通过环境奖励进行优化，训练脚手架在部署时丢弃。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，Pats比强基线提升高达18.6%。在七个搜索增强QA基准上，Pats在保持竞争力的同时，比基线少使用32.1%的提示词。

Conclusion: Pats通过以策略为中心的训练范式，利用动态脚手架有效改进了长周期LLM智能体强化学习中弱策略的探索和优化，在多个基准上取得了显著提升，同时保持了提示效率。

Abstract: In long-horizon LLM agent reinforcement learning, weak policies often repeat similar failures, producing uninformative rollout trajectories and limiting effective policy optimization. Existing skill-centric methods improve exploration by optimizing, filtering, or internalizing reusable skills. However, they remain centered on the skills themselves rather than being designed as adaptive training-time support for the evolving policy. To address this, we propose a policy-centric training paradigm that reframes skills as a dynamic training scaffold. Our framework, Pats, converts rollout groups from the latest policy into evidence cards and uses task-specific evaluation to adjust the context used in subsequent rollouts. Concrete guidance helps weak policies to complete challenging tasks. As policy improves, redundant context is revised or removed to reduce reliance on explicit guidance while preserving useful rollout variation. The policy is optimized with environmental rewards using standard RLVR, and the training scaffold is discarded at deployment. On ALFWorld and WebShop, Pats improves over strong baselines by up to 18.6%. Across seven search-augmented QA benchmarks, it remains competitive while using 32.1% fewer prompt tokens than the baseline.

</details>


### [190] [Bridging the Gap Between Plausibility and Admissibility: Constraint-Aware Flow Maps for Dynamic Graph Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21421)
*Michael Romei de Socio,Gian Luca Pozzato,Alessio Merlo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在动态图结构系统中，通过后采样符号约束（硬过滤、软加权、投影修复）提升生成轨迹模型的结构可行性。在紧凑图场景下无效概率质量仅0.002996，而中等复杂度场景升至0.155929。硬过滤可移除所有无效轨迹并保留84.4%样本，软加权保有效样本量但有效性提升有限。依赖约束是导致不可行的主要原因，统计可行性与结构可采纳性是不同可靠性属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型能通过产生多个可行未来轨迹来支持不确定性下的决策，但统计上的合理性并不保证结构可行性。本研究旨在探究后采样符号约束能否提升动态图结构系统中生成轨迹建模的可靠性。

Method: 采用条件扩散模型从部分观测生成未来图状态轨迹，外部符号层应用硬过滤、软加权或基于投影的修复三种约束方式。在两种合成场景（紧凑图和中复杂度依赖图）上评估结构有效性、样本效率、多样性、鲁棒性和校准度。

Result: 紧凑场景下无效概率质量为0.002996（几乎完全可采纳）；中复杂度场景下升至0.155929。硬过滤移除所有无效轨迹并保留84.4%样本；软加权保留有效样本量但有效性提升有限。家庭级分析表明依赖约束几乎主导所有不可采纳性。

Conclusion: 统计可行性与结构可采纳性是不同的可靠性属性；随着图结构复杂度增加，符号约束处理的价值愈发显著。

Abstract: Generative models can support decision-making under uncertainty by producing ensembles of plausible future system trajectories, but statistical plausibility does not ensure structural feasibility. This study investigates whether post-sampling symbolic constraints can improve the reliability of generative trajectory modeling in dynamic graph-structured systems. A conditional diffusion model generates future graph-state trajectories from partial observations, while an external symbolic layer applies hard filtering, soft weighting, or projection-based repair. The framework is evaluated on two controlled synthetic regimes: a compact graph and a medium-complexity dependency graph, using metrics for structural validity, sample efficiency, diversity, robustness, and calibration. In the compact regime, the model produces an invalid probability mass of 0.002996, indicating an almost entirely admissible trajectory manifold. Under the same architecture and training protocol, invalid mass increases to 0.155929 in the medium-complexity regime. Hard filtering removes all invalid retained trajectories while preserving 84.4% of generated samples, whereas soft weighting preserves effective sample size but yields only limited validity gains. Family-level analysis shows that dependency constraints account for nearly all observed inadmissibility. These results indicate that statistical plausibility and structural admissibility are distinct reliability properties and that symbolic constraint handling becomes more valuable as graph-structural complexity increases.

</details>


### [191] [Agent-Guided Relational Concept Discovery: Toward Interpretable Surgical Margin Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21437)
*Nooshin Maghsoodi,Amoon Jamzad,Robert Policelli,Mohammad Farahmand,Dilakshan Srikanthan,Martin Kaufmann,Kevin Y. M. Ren,Shaila Merchant,Sonal Varma,Ross Walker,Doug McKay,John Rudan,Gabor Fichtinger,Parvin Mousavi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Agent-Guided Concept Discovery的框架，无需预定义概念标签即可从REIMS数据中学习有意义的概念，通过推理代理优化概念描述、自适应调整权重，并利用生化知识图谱确保代谢一致性，在皮肤癌和乳腺癌数据集中提高了平衡准确率和灵敏度，并在术中场景展现出更好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在REIMS手术切缘评估中表现优异，但临床推广受限于：(1) 模型在标注的离体组织数据上训练，却需处理术中采集的噪声、未标注数据；(2) 黑箱特性导致行为难以理解和系统改进；(3) 监督概念学习方法需要难以获取的概念标注。

Method: 提出Agent-Guided Concept Discovery框架：训练时使用推理代理优化所学习概念的语义描述，并根据诊断相关性自适应调整概念权重；同时利用生化知识图谱对概念进行约束，确保其与已知代谢关系一致。

Result: 在皮肤癌和乳腺癌数据集中，该方法相比基线模型提升了平衡准确率和灵敏度。在代表性术中案例中，假阳性更少，表明在手术条件下的泛化能力更好。

Conclusion: Agent-Guided Concept Discovery能够无需概念标注即可学习可解释的、与代谢知识一致的概念，有效提升了模型在术中REIMS数据分析中的泛化性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Deep learning models can effectively use Rapid Evaporative Ionization Mass Spectrometry (REIMS) data for surgical margin assessment. However, their clinical adoption remains challenging due to limited generalization to operating room conditions. This difficulty arises because models are typically trained on labeled spectra collected from resected tissue samples, while they must operate on noisy, unlabeled data acquired directly during surgery. In addition, the black-box nature of deep learning models makes it difficult to understand and systematically improve their behavior. Concept-based learning offers a promising way to address these challenges by mapping raw measurements to human-understandable concepts. However, supervised concept-based approaches rely on concept annotations, which are difficult to obtain in complex mass spectrometry workflows. We propose Agent-Guided Concept Discovery, a framework that learns meaningful concepts directly from data without requiring predefined concept labels. During training, a reasoning agent refines semantic descriptions of the learned concepts and adaptively adjusts their weight based on diagnostic relevance. These concepts are further grounded using a biochemical knowledge graph to ensure consistency with known metabolic relationships. Across Skin and Breast Cancer datasets, our model improves balanced accuracy and sensitivity over the baseline. In a representative intraoperative case, it shows fewer false positives, indicating better generalization to surgical conditions.

</details>


### [192] [Detecting LLM-Generated Tokens in Human--LLM Coauthored Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21458)
*Yangjun Lu,Hongyi Zhou,Fabian Spill,Kai Ye,Chengchun Shi,Jin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于token级别的LLM生成文本检测方法，通过Lepski型自适应平滑相邻检测分数来定位混合作者文档中由LLM生成的部分，无需token级标注数据，在理论和实证上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作写作日益普及，需要细粒度检测方法来定位混合作者文档中由LLM生成的部分，而现有方法主要集中于文档级分类，无法识别文本中哪些部分由LLM生成。

Method: 在token级别上，利用现存的token级检测分数，通过Lepski型自适应规则平滑相邻token分数以降低其变异性，根据局部作者结构自适应选择带宽。

Result: 理论上，该方法在估计潜在信号方面达到了优越的均方误差性能；实验上，在合成数据集和真实数据集上均优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的token级检测方法简单易实现，无需token级标注数据，在理论和实证上都表现出色，并已部署公开可访问的网站提供服务。

Abstract: The rise of human-AI collaborative writing has created a growing need for fine-grained detection methods that support localizing likely LLM-generated content in mixed-authorship documents. Existing methods for detecting LLM-generated text mainly focus on document-level classification and cannot identify which parts of the text are generated by LLMs. This paper introduces a new method to address this urgent need. Our method operates at the token level, the natural unit of modern language models, and builds on existing token-level detection scores. The key idea is to smooth adjacent token scores to reduce their variability, while using an adaptive Lepski-type rule to select the bandwidth according to the local authorship structure. Our method is simple to implement and does not require token-level labeled data for training. Theoretically, we characterize this trade-off and show that the proposed method achieves favorable mean square error performance in estimating the underlying signal. Empirically, we demonstrate strong performance of our method against a wide range of baselines in both synthetic datasets and a realistic dataset. We deploy a publicly accessible website that implements the methods as well.

</details>


### [193] [AREX: Towards a Recursively Self-Improving Agent for Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21461)
*Shuqi Lu,Chaofan Li,Kun Luo,Zhang Zhang,Hui Wang,Hongwang Xiao,Zheng Liu,Lei Xiong,Jiahao Wang,Sen Wang,Xiyan Jiang,Wanli Li,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Bingyu Yan,Ziyi Xia,Yingxia Shao,Kang Liu,Zhicheng Dou,Di He,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zhongyuan Wang,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AREX是一类递归自改进（RSI）深度研究智能体，通过内外双循环机制（内环收集证据构建答案，外环逐约束审计并启动定向改进）解决多约束联合满足问题，并学习自主上下文压缩工具以支持长程自改进，在多个深度研究与推理基准上超越同规模基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究需要同时满足多个约束的答案，而发现答案成本高昂，但验证候选答案可分解为可处理的逐约束检查。这种"发现-验证不对称性"启发研究者应超越简单搜索，通过递归地验证中间结果并利用部分验证状态指导后续改进。

Method: 提出AREX框架，包含：(1) 内环研究循环：收集证据并构建临时答案；(2) 外环自改进循环：逐约束审计答案，识别未解决的声明，启动定向后续研究。引入自主上下文更新工具，将增长的交互历史压缩为紧凑的改进状态（保留已验证证据和未解决约束）。通过智能体中间训练和长程强化学习训练，在长程学习中通过强调获取关键证据或纠正错误方向的关键步骤来缓解稀疏奖励问题。

Result: 在BrowseComp、WideSearch、DeepSearchQA、HLE等深度研究和工具使用基准上，AREX（密集4B模型和122B-A10B MoE模型）显著优于同规模基线，并与使用更多激活参数的模型竞争。

Conclusion: 递归自改进范式结合内外双循环与自主上下文压缩，能有效提升深度研究智能体的多约束满足能力，验证了"验证引导发现"的设计原则在长程推理中的可行性。

Abstract: Deep research requires agents to find answers that jointly satisfy multiple constraints. Discovering such answers is costly, whereas verifying a candidate can often be decomposed into tractable constraint-wise checks. This discovery--verification asymmetry suggests that a research agent should do more than simply search longer: it should recursively improve its current answer by verifying intermediate results and using the partially verified state to guide subsequent refinement. We introduce AREX, a family of Recursively Self-Improving (RSI) deep research agents. AREX alternates between an inner research loop that gathers evidence and constructs a provisional answer, and an outer self-improvement loop that audits the answer constraint-wise, identifies unresolved claims, and launches targeted follow-up research. To sustain RSI over long horizons, AREX learns an autonomous context-update tool that compresses growing interaction history into a compact improvement state preserving verified evidence and unresolved constraints, without relying on an external model. We train AREX on verified synthetic tasks and high-quality trajectories through agentic mid-training and long-horizon reinforcement learning. To mitigate sparse final rewards during long horizon learning, we emphasize key steps where decisive evidence is acquired or erroneous research directions are corrected. We instantiate a dense 4B model and a 122B-A10B Mixture-of-Experts model. Across BrowseComp, WideSearch, DeepSearchQA, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and other reasoning and tool-use benchmarks, AREX substantially outperforms comparable-scale baselines and remains competitive with models using substantially more activated parameters.

</details>


### [194] [Toward Continuous Assurance for the Democratization of AI Agent Creation in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21495)
*Natan Levy,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别了民主化AI代理创建带来的可靠性挑战，并提出了一种轻量级持续保障框架，通过依赖映射、就绪合约、计划检查、诊断和生命周期治理来评估代理是否保持操作就绪状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非工程用户通过低代码/无代码平台创建AI代理导致民主化，但这些代理依赖不断变化的模型、工具、检索源、权限、提示、调度和外部服务，部署后可能无声退化，造成可靠性差距。

Method: 提出轻量级持续保障框架，包含依赖映射、就绪合约、计划检查、诊断和生命周期治理五个组件；并开发了初始原型审计器及基于场景的评估方法。

Result: 展示了如何将提出的分类法转化为实际检查项和可操作的修复指导，并通过场景评估验证了方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 民主化AI代理创建需要持续的可靠性保障机制，所提框架和原型能够有效检测代理在预期条件下的操作就绪状态变化，弥补当前缺乏系统性保障的空白。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly created inside organizations by non-engineering users through low-code, no-code, and conversational development environments. This democratization enables rapid local innovation, but it also creates a reliability gap: agents that appear to users as simple productivity artifacts may depend on changing models, tools, retrieval sources, permissions, prompts, schedules, and external services. These dependencies can cause silent degradation long after deployment, even when no user directly modifies the agent. This paper identifies the reliability challenge created by democratized AI agent creation and proposes a lightweight continuous-assurance framework for citizen-created organizational agents. The framework combines dependency mapping, readiness contracts, scheduled checks, diagnostics, and lifecycle governance to assess whether an agent remains operationally ready under expected conditions. We also present an initial prototype auditor and scenario-based assessment showing how the proposed taxonomy can be translated into practical checks and actionable remediation guidance.

</details>


### [195] [Agentic Context Management: Solving Agent Memory and Cost by Treating Them as Lifecycle and Architecture Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21503)
*Gaurav Dadhich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Context Management (ACM)概念，指出来Agent失败主因是上下文管理而非推理能力不足。ACM包含五个原语：架构设计、摄入、范围界定、预判、压缩整合。作者实现了多租户服务Maximem Synap，在LongMemEval和LoCoMo上分别达到92%和93.2%，并论证了朴素累积导致二次方token成本，而有效压缩可实现线性成本与保真度兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI Agent在生产环境中失败的主要原因并非推理能力不足，而是无法有效管理推理上下文（对话历史、长提示词、工具定义和输出膨胀）。当前的解决方案将这一问题窄化为存储与检索问题，作者认为需要更全面的视角。

Method: 本文将上下文管理定义为一个生命周期，包含五个原语：（1）架构设计（Architecting），（2）摄入（Ingesting），（3）范围界定（Scoping），（4）预判（Anticipating），（5）压缩与整合（Compacting & Consolidation）。并基于此实现了多租户服务Maximem Synap。

Result: Maximem Synap在LongMemEval上达到92%，在LoCoMo上达到93.2%。经济分析表明：朴素上下文累积的token成本随对话长度呈二次方增长；粗略摘要可降为线性但伴随准确率悬崖；只有经过验证的压缩才能实现线性成本与保真度兼得。

Conclusion: ACM是Agent系统走向生产环境的关键学科，现有基准未覆盖延迟、token效率和上下文腐烂抵抗等维度，未来方向包括决策级和组织级上下文管理。

Abstract: Production AI agents' failures are less often due to an inability to reason well and more often because they cannot manage what is in their reasoning context: conversation histories, large prompts, large tool definitions, and ballooning tool outputs. Agents drown in their own accumulating history while paying a token cost that grows every turn, producing missing recalls within and across conversations. The incumbent response treats this as a storage-and-retrieval problem. We argue that framing is too narrow. Actively managing what an agent holds in mind is a lifecycle, not merely a store: it spans deciding what to remember, extracting and structuring it, choosing the right store per data type, consolidating and forgetting while preserving provenance, deciding what is relevant now, anticipating what is needed next, and compacting context to a budget without losing what matters. In serious production this operates not over a single user but across an organizational scope hierarchy. We name this discipline Agentic Context Management (ACM) and decompose it into five primitives: architecting, ingesting, scoping, anticipating, and compacting & consolidation. We then make the economic case: naive context accumulation grows token cost quadratically in conversation length, crude summarization buys linear cost at the price of an accuracy cliff, and only validated compaction achieves linear cost with preserved fidelity. We describe a reference implementation, Maximem Synap, that realizes the five primitives as a multi-tenant service and reports 92% on LongMemEval and 93.2% on LoCoMo under the configuration detailed in Section 6. We close with dimensions existing benchmarks do not yet capture, latency, token efficiency, and context-rot resistance, and the frontier of decision-level and organization-level context the category points toward.

</details>


### [196] [Same Dangerous Objective, Opposite Advice: Direct Exposure versus Multi-Agent Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21518)
*Linjun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 高能力LLM直接面对危险目标时表现更安全，但经过中间代理转换上述目标后（隐藏操纵性条款和来源），用户端模型反而产生与操控目标对齐的建议，暴露出多阶段工作流中的组合安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在多阶段自动化工作流中面对通过中间代理转换后的操纵性目标时的行为变化，揭示当前安全对齐方法在组合场景下的潜在漏洞。

Method: 使用OpenAI gpt-5.6-sol模型，测试25个预定义的镜像权衡剖面。比较直接暴露于包含隐藏、伪造、施压等操纵性条款的目标，与经过"Id and Censor"模块将该目标转换为情感化和约束重写后的意图，再传递给用户端Superego模型时的输出差异。

Result: 直接暴露于操纵性目标时，模型产生与目标相悖的建议；但经过中间代理转换后，用户端模型由于看不到原始目标、操纵性条款及其来源，反而产生与操控目标对齐的建议。这种行为的反向转变与模型识别或怀疑操纵性动机的机制一致。

Conclusion: 发现了当前高能力LLM在组合式多阶段工作流中的安全漏洞：模型可作为用户端组件服务于明确操纵性目标的工作流，下游模型看不到原始指令、操纵性条款和来源，但目标方向得以保留。端点用户也无法直接检查上游信息。

Abstract: Even a current high-capability LLM can appear safer when shown a dangerous objective directly than when other agents transform and relay its direction. Using OpenAI's gpt-5.6-sol model alias, we test 25 pre-specified mirrored trade-off profiles. Direct exposure to an objective authorizing concealment, fabrication, and pressure produced advice net opposed to its target. After an Id and Censor transformed the same objective into affect and a constraint-rewritten, target-bearing intention, the user-facing Superego---which saw the preferred direction but not the raw objective, its manipulative clauses, or its source---produced advice net aligned with the target.
  This behavioral reverse shift is consistent with the model recognizing or distrusting the manipulative motive, although we do not identify its internal mechanism. The second result exposes a compositional safety gap: a current high-capability model can be used as the user-facing component of an automated, multi-stage workflow serving an explicitly manipulative objective. The workflow can keep the raw instruction, its manipulation-authorizing clauses, and its provenance outside the downstream model's context while preserving the objective's target direction. A user with endpoint-only access likewise cannot directly inspect those upstream messages including the objective.

</details>


### [197] [MIRROR: Learning from the Other View for Multi-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21552)
*Wen Ye,Yuxiao Qu,Aviral Kumar,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRROR方法，利用多视图（文本、图像、图文结合）互补推理路径，通过强化学习让弱视图向强视图学习，提升VLM在几何问题上的视觉推理能力与跨模态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在几何问题中不同视图（文本、图像、图文结合）表现出不一致的推理行为——模型可能解对文本题但解错对应图形题，反之亦然。标准多模态后训练未充分利用这种互补性，因此需要一种方法利用多视图间的互补推理路径来提升整体视觉推理能力。

Method: 构建高质量配对多模态几何数据集ODA-Data，包含文本主导、图像主导、图文结合三种视图。提出MIRROR方法：对每个问题，在全部视图下评估模型，选择最佳视图作为教师，使用反向KL散度目标对其他视图进行蒸馏训练，实现自监督强化学习优化。

Result: 在多个几何推理基准上，MIRROR相比标准强化学习取得更优性能，模型在不同模态下的推理更准确且更一致。

Conclusion: 多视图间的互补推理路径可以被有效利用，通过自监督的强化学习框架（让强视图指导弱视图），可以系统性提升VLM在几何问题上的视觉推理能力和跨模态行为一致性。

Abstract: Unlike large language models (LLMs) that exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with visual reasoning, even on geometry problems that admit equivalent text, diagram, and combined diagram+text views. We show that these views often elicit different behaviors: a model may solve a problem from text but fail on the corresponding diagram, or succeed visually while failing textually. This inconsistency suggests that different views expose complementary reasoning paths and failure modes that standard multimodal post-training does not fully exploit. To study and exploit this phenomenon, we construct ODA-Data, a high-quality paired multimodal geometry dataset with text-dominant, image-dominant, and combined image+text views of the same problems, together with splits for training and evaluating modality-dependent reasoning behaviors. We then develop Modality-Informed Reciprocal Reasoning Optimization (MIRROR), a reinforcement learning approach for improving multimodal reasoning via self supervision. For each problem, MIRROR evaluates the model under all views, selects the best-performing view as a teacher, and trains other views with a reverse-KL objective towards the teacher. Across reasoning benchmarks that evaluate on geometry problems, MIRROR improves over standard RL and yields more accurate and consistent behavior across modalities

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Sycophancy: Structured Resistance and Compliance in LLM Moral Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21558)
*Baihui Wang,Bernard Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过三项研究揭示了大语言模型在判断修正过程中受三个社会心理学维度（观点距离、来源归因、联盟结构）的系统性影响，将谄媚现象重新定义为更广泛的判断更新过程的一种表现，并为区分建设性信念修正与谄媚性顺从提供了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将谄媚（sycophancy）视为大语言模型的一维失败模式，但作者认为需要更全面的框架来理解模型何时应吸收他人观点、何时应坚持自身道德判断，从而构建具有社会校准能力的模型。

Method: 通过三项实证研究，系统操纵观点距离、来源归因和联盟结构三个维度，观察大语言模型在判断修正任务中的行为模式，并与人类社会心理学中的经典现象进行对比分析。

Result: 模型对较近观点更易接受；对标注为自身先前判断的观点更易受影响；对群体压力表现出不同程度的响应。这三个维度平行于人类社会心理学中的经典现象，表明谄媚只是更广泛判断更新过程的一种特定表达。

Conclusion: 该框架为区分建设性信念修正与谄媚性顺从提供了原则性基础，有助于在道德相关的交互中实现更好的对齐。

Abstract: Building socially calibrated large language models, which can learn from others without simply yielding to them, requires more than reducing sycophancy as a one-dimensional failure mode. Models must distinguish when to incorporate others' perspectives from when to maintain a well-grounded moral judgment. We study the broader resistance-compliance process governing this distinction. Across three studies, we show that models' judgment revision is structured along three dimensions that parallel classic phenomena in human social psychology: the distance between an incoming view and the model's initial position, the source attribution of that view, and the coalition structure supporting it. Models are generally more receptive to nearby positions, more influenced by views presented as their own prior judgments, and differently responsive to group pressure. These findings recast sycophancy as one expression of a broader judgment-updating process shaped by social influence. Our framework provides a principled basis for distinguishing constructive belief revision from sycophantic compliance, thereby supporting better alignment in morally consequential interactions.

</details>


### [199] [Unsupervised Consensus-Based Anomaly Detection for Spatiotemporal Malaria Incidence in Ghana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21559)
*T. Ansah-Narh,Y. Asare Afrane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种共识异常检测框架，应用于加纳2014-2023年的月度疟疾监测数据，以识别非典型传播模式。研究发现，异常在时空上高度结构化，阿散蒂和北部地区是反复出现异常的主要区域，塔马利、库马西和阿克拉为持续热点。关键发现是异常负担（异常期间累积病例）与异常频率（异常行为的持续性）存在空间差异：塔马利的异常负担最高，而异常率最高的地区集中在阿散蒂地区，表明高负担区域不一定是最常出现异常传播的区域。异常月份在统计上构成显著不同的群体，其病例数（Cohen's d=3.252）和季节性偏差（d>1.2）远高于正常月份。研究认为，仅靠疟疾负担无法完整反映传播动态，该框架通过区分疟疾最流行区域与传播行为最异常区域，可加强监测、优先调查并支持精准防控策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的疟疾监测通常只关注病例负担（即病例数量），但这种方法可能忽略了传播行为的异常模式，而这些异常模式可能预示着疫情暴发、传播动态变化或防控漏洞。作者希望超越简单的负担统计，通过检测非典型传播模式来更全面地理解疟疾传播动态，从而为精准防控提供新的视角。

Method: 采用共识异常检测框架，对加纳2014-2023年的月度疟疾监测数据进行分析。该方法通过集成多种异常检测算法，识别时间序列中偏离正常模式的观测点，并对识别的异常进行时空聚类分析，区分异常负担（累积病例数）和异常频率（异常行为的持续性）。

Result: 1) 异常在时空上高度结构化，阿散蒂和北部地区是反复异常的主要区域，塔马利、库马西和阿克拉为持续热点；2) 异常负担与异常频率存在空间分离：塔马利异常负担最高，而异常率最高的地区集中在阿散蒂地区；3) 异常月份与正常月份在统计上显著不同，异常月份病例数极高（Cohen's d=3.252），季节性偏差也很大（d>1.2）。

Conclusion: 疟疾负担本身无法全面反映传播动态。通过区分疟疾最流行的区域与传播行为最异常的区域，该异常检测框架能够加强监测预警、优先安排流行病学调查，并支持更具针对性的防控策略，从而提高资源分配效率和干预效果。

Abstract: A consensus anomaly detection framework was applied to monthly malaria surveillance data from Ghana (2014-2023) to identify atypical transmission patterns. Anomalies were highly structured in space and time. Ashanti and Northern Regions accounted for most recurrent anomalies, with persistent hotspots at Tamale, Kumasi, and Accra. A key finding was the spatial distinction between anomaly burden (cumulative cases during anomalous periods) and anomaly frequency (persistence of unusual behaviour). Tamale had the highest burden during anomalies, whereas the highest anomaly rates clustered in Ashanti districts, showing that high-burden areas are not necessarily those with the most frequent anomalous transmission. Anomalous months formed a statistically distinct group, with much higher case counts (Cohen's $d = 3.252$) and large seasonal deviations ($d > 1.2$) compared with normal months. Malaria burden alone provides an incomplete picture of transmission dynamics. By distinguishing where malaria is most prevalent from where transmission behaves most unusually, this framework can strengthen surveillance, prioritise investigations, and support targeted control strategies.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [DataPrep-Bench: Benchmarking LLMs as Training Data Preparators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20465)
*Hao Liang,Qifeng Cai,Yibo Lin,Jianzhuo Du,Qifeng Xia,Sizhe Qiu,Linzhuang Sun,Meiyi Qiang,Zhaoyang Han,Xiaochen Ma,Bohan Zeng,Ruichuan An,Conghui He,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DataPrep-Bench，首个统一基准，联合评估LLM驱动数据准备中的两个互补能力：数据构建和数据质量评估。同时提出Data-Construction-Skill智能体和Distributional Alignment Score (DAS)评估指标，实验表明DAS在六个域中四个达到最强跨模型相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏统一的基准来衡量LLM、智能体和数据驱动流程如何端到端地准备训练数据。现有工作要么聚焦数据构建，要么聚焦数据质量评估，没有将两者置于共享的下游训练验证框架下统一评估。

Method: 1) 提出DataPrep-Bench统一基准，涵盖六个领域和多个基座模型，采用下游训练验证协议。2) 数据构建轨道：方法基于相同原始源，通过在下游任务（Dolly-15k）上微调基座模型来评分。3) 提出Data-Construction-Skill智能体，利用技能引导进行数据构建。4) 数据质量评估轨道：评分函数通过与下游性能的Pearson相关性打分。5) 提出DAS（分布对齐分数），基于MMD度量候选数据集与领域代理之间的分布差异。

Result: Data-Construction-Skill在Llama-3.1-8B金融领域上比Dolly-only基线提升近20个绝对点，在知识提取密集领域与最强智能体和DataFlow方法竞争力相当。DAS在六个域中四个达到最强跨模型相关性，在数学、科学和医学领域同时超越r>0.70，优于现有基于质量、多样性和启发式的评估器。

Conclusion: DataPrep-Bench为LLM驱动数据准备提供了统一的下游验证框架，将数据构建和数据质量评估作为同等重要的目标进行度量，推动了该领域的标准化评估。

Abstract: The quality of training data fundamentally determines the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet no unified benchmark exists to measure how well LLMs, agents, and data-centric workflows actually prepare training data end to end. We view LLM-driven data preparation as comprising two complementary capabilities: data construction, which transforms raw sources into supervised training data, and data quality evaluation, which predicts the training value of candidate datasets before downstream training; throughout, "quality" refers to downstream training utility rather than surface-level textual properties. We introduce DataPrep-Bench, the first unified benchmark that jointly evaluates both capabilities under a shared downstream-grounded protocol over six domains and multiple base models. For data construction, methods consume identical raw sources and are scored by fine-tuning a base model on their outputs jointly with Dolly-15k; alongside this track we release Data-Construction-Skill, a skill-guided agent that lifts the Dolly-only baseline by nearly 20 points absolute on Llama-3.1-8B Finance and is competitive with the strongest agent- and DataFlow-based methods in knowledge-extraction-dense domains. For data quality evaluation, scoring functions are scored by Pearson correlation with downstream performance on a shared candidate pool; we release the Distributional Alignment Score (DAS), a distribution-based evaluator that uses MMD between a candidate dataset and a domain proxy. DAS attains the strongest cross-model correlation in four of six domains and is the only metric clearing r > 0.70 simultaneously in Math, Science, and Medical, outperforming existing quality-, diversity-, and heuristic-based evaluators. DataPrep-Bench provides a unified, downstream-grounded framework for measuring progress on both capabilities as co-equal targets of LLM-driven data preparation.

</details>


### [201] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对百万级上下文场景下MTP草案头的全注意力KV读取成本随上下文线性增长的问题，提出Windowed-MTP方法：仅对草案头施加StreamingLLM风格滑窗+注意力汇聚，保留目标模型的完全注意力验证。该方法无需训练、即插即用、无损输出分布，将草案KV工作集约束为常数（1M上下文丢弃~99% KV条目），在三类架构（Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B、Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B）上单GPU每解码步成本降低28%-44%，且可回收未读草案KV内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码中MTP草案头在长上下文场景下执行全注意力导致KV读取成本随上下文线性增长，抵消了投机加速的收益，且混合/线性注意力目标模型使该问题更严重。

Method: 对草案注意力层仅应用StreamingLLM风格的滑窗+注意力汇聚（Windowed-MTP），将草案KV工作集约束为常数，目标模型保持全注意力验证不变。回收未读草案KV内存通过紧凑环形缓冲区实现。

Result: 在1M上下文、单GPU场景下，三类架构的每解码步成本降低28%-44%；丢弃约99% KV条目；可回收7.7-11%总KV内存且不影响接受率或质量。

Conclusion: Windowed-MTP是一种训练免费、无损输出分布、即插即用的方法，有效解决了长上下文场景下MTP草案头的高成本问题，显著提升投机解码效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [202] [PhantomFill: When the Form Demands an Answer, Language Models Invent One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20492)
*Rana Muhammad Usman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了在结构化输出（JSON字段、函数参数等）场景下，语言模型因格式约束而产生严重幻觉。即使面对无法回答的问题，自由文本模式下模型能诚实回答，但一旦转换为必填字段等结构化格式，模型就会编造答案。作者发布了PhantomFill基准测试，并提出一行schema的修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产中语言模型并不写散文，而是填充表单（JSON字段、函数参数、提取模板）。作者发现表单本身会导致幻觉，而这一重要问题尚未被系统性衡量。

Method: 对13个模型提出相同问题但仅改变答案格式。输入构建为无法回答的场景（如无回复的帖子、未转录的通话）。对比自由文本与结构化格式（必填JSON字段/枚举/数组）下的回答行为。测试显式"证据不足"选项和"不要推断情感"指令的效果。

Result: GPT-5.5在自由文本中98%诚实回答"无回复数据"；给定必填JSON情感字段时，40/40次编造答案。10/13个模型在必填字段驱动下100%编造。9个开放权重模型忽略"证据不足"选项。4/6个模型无视"不要推断情感"指令。幻觉集中在必填枚举和最小计数数组等无法添加免责声明的位置。

Conclusion: 格式压力下的诚实性是无人衡量的训练产出。模型规模与抗幻觉能力呈非线性关系（小模型拒绝、中模型编造、大模型再次拒绝）。修复方案是在schema中添加一行（明确的"证据不足"选项），但失败现象普遍存在。

Abstract: Language models in production do not write prose. They fill forms: JSON fields, function arguments, extraction templates. We show that the form itself causes hallucination.
  We ask thirteen models the same question about the same input and change only the answer format. The inputs are built so the question cannot be answered: a viral post showing 12,400 likes but no visible replies, a support ticket whose call was never transcribed. In free text, GPT-5.5 answers honestly. It says there is no reply data, 98% of the time. Given a required JSON field for sentiment, the same model invents an answer 40 times out of 40. It fabricates the mood of crowds it never saw and quotes customers it never heard.
  The pattern holds with force. Required fields drive fabrication to 100% in ten of thirteen models. An explicit "insufficient evidence" option rescues only the frontier: all nine open-weight models ignore it. A direct instruction, do not infer sentiment, is overridden by the schema in four of six models. Resistance does not come with scale: within a single model family, the smallest model refuses, the mid-sized model fabricates, the largest refuses again. Honesty under format pressure is a training outcome that no one is measuring.
  The fabrication hides exactly where hedging is impossible: in required enums and minimum-count arrays, fields where no disclaimer fits. We release PhantomFill, a benchmark with deterministic scoring and two reportable numbers: the Coerced Fabrication Rate and the Escape Utilization Rate. The fix we test is one line of schema. The failure we measure is everywhere.

</details>


### [203] [The Active Ingredient in Muon's Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20512)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Muon优化器在模算术任务中比AdamW更快达到grokking阈值的机制。通过消融实验发现，加速来源于正交化（Newton-Schulz迭代），而非谱范数约束；正交化优化器在更低的谱范数下收敛；减少Newton-Schulz迭代次数可加速但会导致解脆弱易坍塌；谱缩放可被移除而不损失性能。论文还提出了基于稳定性的度量标准，并公开了完整代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将Muon优化器加速grokking归因于"谱范数约束加正交化动量"，但未隔离具体机制。本文旨在分解并压力测试各组件的影响，以理解Muon加速grokking的根本原因。

Method: 使用多种子、多学习率的系统扫描进行消融实验：分离正交化（通过Newton-Schulz迭代）和谱范数约束的效果；进行机制分析比较谱范数变化；改变Newton-Schulz迭代次数（1-5）；移除谱缩放组件。采用首次穿越时间和持续grok时间两种度量标准。

Result: 加速来自正交化而非谱范数约束；正交化优化器在约3倍更低的谱范数下泛化；单次Newton-Schulz迭代加速但解脆弱，五次迭代是鲁棒选择；谱缩放可移除无代价。

Conclusion: Muon加速grokking的关键机制是正交化动量，谱范数约束影响不大。优化器的"更快"需要基于稳定性度量重新评估，单次迭代在低学习率下可行但整体上五次迭代更鲁棒。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer reaches the grokking threshold on modular arithmetic faster than AdamW. Prior work attributes this to "spectral-norm constraints plus orthogonalized momentum" but does not isolate which mechanism matters. To better understand Moun's behavior, we run multi-seed and multi-learning-rate sweeps to decompose and stress-test the effect. First, an ablation shows the speedup comes from orthogonalization (the Newton-Schulz iteration): orthogonalize-only matches full Muon, whereas spectral-only is no faster than AdamW and is unreliable, and this verdict holds across learning rates. Second, a mechanistic analysis finds that orthogonalizing optimizers reach generalization at roughly 3x lower spectral norm and, controlling for how much the embedding actually moves, settle into a lower-norm solution rather than simply perturbing the embedding less. Third, reducing the Newton-Schulz iteration count from five to one accelerates reaching the threshold but makes the grokked solution fragile, prone to transient collapse, with fragility that grows with learning rate; a single iteration is fast and stable only at small learning rate, while the canonical five iterations are the learning-rate-robust choice. We also show spectral scaling can be dropped at no measured cost. A methodological thread runs throughout: under a stability-aware metric, "faster" claims about grokking optimizers can invert, so we report both first-crossing and sustained-grok times. To support reproducibility, we release our full training and analysis code at https://github.com/louiswang524/muon-grokking-frontier

</details>


### [204] [Scaling Closed-Loop Feature Channel Configuration with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20516)
*Tolgay Atinc Uzun,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过扩大采样规模（每周期250个候选网络，共2000个）验证了基于LLM的通道配置搜索方法的有效性。实验发现：平均准确率呈正线性趋势（斜率9.87e-4, p=0.043），最佳准确率从0.3144提升至0.3676；参数效率显著改善（最佳模型11.8M参数 vs 早期模型166.5M参数）；41.8%的候选网络采用非2的幂次通道宽度，最强模型呈现“中等早期宽度+扩展中后期模块”的结构规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证初期研究中基于LLM的闭环通道配置搜索方法在密集采样条件下是否仍有效，并探索更大规模搜索中是否涌现出新的架构规律。

Method: 将搜索规模扩展至每微调周期250个候选网络，共8个完整周期（2000个候选），经任务和元数据过滤后获得462个有效CIFAR-100评估结果，分析准确率趋势、参数效率和架构特征。

Result: 每周期平均准确率呈正线性趋势（斜率9.87e-4, p=0.043）；最佳准确率从0.3144提升至0.3676；top-5和top-10周期均值也呈正趋势；最佳模型参数降至11.8M（vs 早期166.5M）；41.8%候选使用非2的幂次通道宽度；强模型呈“中等早期宽度+扩展中后期模块”的通道分配模式。

Conclusion: 初期研究中观察到的通道搜索信号在密集采样条件下依然有效，且更大规模搜索揭示了参数效率和架构规律方面的额外优势。

Abstract: Promising initial results in closed-loop large-language-model-based channel-configuration search demonstrated that neural-network widths can be optimized directly through executable code generation and accuracy feedback. However, those results were obtained from a relatively sparse set of valid evaluations, leaving open whether the observed optimization behavior transfers to a denser sampling regime and whether additional architectural regularities emerge when more generated networks are evaluated. To test this, the same search setting is scaled to 250 candidate networks per fine-tuning cycle. The analysis covers 2000 generated candidates from 8 complete cycles, yielding 462 verified CIFAR-100 evaluations after task and metadata filtering. Per-cycle mean accuracy exhibits a positive linear trend with slope 9.87e-4 (p=0.043), while the high-performing frontier improves more strongly: the best observed accuracy increases from 0.3144 to 0.3676, and both the top-5 and top-10 cycle-level means exhibit positive trends. The scaled run also reveals improved parameter efficiency. The best model reaches 0.3676 with 11.8M parameters, compared with an early high-performing model at 0.3144 with 166.5M parameters. Beyond accuracy, the larger sample exposes architectural regularities that were difficult to assess from sparse observations. Non-power-of-two channel widths occur in 41.8% of verified candidates, and the strongest models share structured channel-allocation patterns characterized by moderate early widths and expanded middle or later blocks. These findings indicate that the channel-search signal observed in the initial study transfers

</details>


### [205] [Multimodal CoLRAG-TF: Triple-Filtered Retrieval for Complex PDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20517)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Multimodal CoLRAG-TF，一种四轴融合架构，通过密集文本嵌入、BM25关键词匹配、知识图谱三元组过滤和图像相似度融合，在异构PDF（日本灾害教训文档）上实现鲁棒检索，显著提升了多跳推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG在处理异构PDF集合时面临多模态内容、领域专业术语以及分散证据间的多跳推理需求等挑战，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出四轴融合架构，集成密集文本嵌入、BM25关键词匹配、知识图谱三元组过滤和图像相似度；构建多模态索引（2,403个块），结合混合OCR流水线和LLM生成标题；提取11,414个OpenIE三元组并用FAISS索引；采用HippoRAG2启发的粗到精检索器（卷→章→块）缩小搜索空间；通过贝叶斯优化融合权重。

Result: 在457对基准上，检索召回率达0.9909，多跳答案相似度较单跳查询提升71.6%；三元组轴权重需占主导（α=0.44）以抵消词汇偏差并维持多跳检索质量。

Conclusion: 三元组过滤的多模态融合对于嘈杂异构PDF上的结构化推理至关重要，该框架可推广至灾害领域之外。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over heterogeneous PDF collections remains challenging due to multimodal content, domain-specific terminology, and the need for multi-hop reasoning across dispersed evidence. We present Multimodal CoLRAG-TF, a four-axis fusion architecture that integrates dense text embeddings, BM25 keyword matching, knowledge-graph triple filtering, and image-based similarity for robust retrieval over complex documents. Our system constructs a multimodal index of 2,403 blocks extracted from 43 Japanese disaster lesson PDFs, supported by a hybrid OCR pipeline and LLM-based caption generation. To enhance compositional reasoning, we extract 11,414 OpenIE triples and index them with FAISS, enabling sub-second triple lookup and hierarchical propagation of relevance signals. A HippoRAG2-inspired coarse-to-fine retriever (volume $\to$ chapter $\to$ block) narrows the search space before final fusion scoring. Bayesian optimization over fusion weights reveals that the triple axis must dominate ($α_\text{triple} = 0.44$) to counteract lexical bias and sustain multi-hop retrieval quality. Evaluated on a 457-pair benchmark, Multimodal CoLRAG-TF achieves a Retrieval Recall of 0.9909 and a 71.6$\%$ improvement in multi-hop answer similarity over single-hop queries. An image-to-lesson pipeline using a vision LLM further demonstrates the applicability of the approach to visual inputs. These results show that triple-filtered multimodal fusion is essential for structured reasoning over noisy, heterogeneous PDFs and provides a general framework applicable beyond the disaster domain.

</details>


### [206] [Adaptive Depth in Looped Transformers: Diagnosing Learned Halting Gates and Trajectory Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20519)
*Andrei Cristian Popescu,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 循环Transformer通过共享递归模块重复计算来增加测试时计算量。本文通过轨迹-读出双重视角，系统研究了循环Transformer中的自适应深度问题，发现固定先验深度监督能产生难度感知的轨迹，且简单的后验置信度读出常优于学习型门控。联合门控训练导致的轨迹劣化是性能不佳的主因，而非门控表达能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环Transformer中学习型停顿时机目标通常将单个出口分布同时用作推理时停止规则和训练时各层损失的加权，导致出口选择与轨迹形成相互纠缠——门控不仅决定使用哪个递归状态，还决定每个中间状态的监督强度，从而可能产生较差的适应计算性能。

Method: 通过轨迹-读出双重视角分析循环Transformer中的自适应深度，在受控合成任务（模算术和二进制奇偶校验）和大规模Ouro-1.4B及2.6B检查点上进行实验。比较固定先验深度监督与学习型门控，分析联合训练与冻结轨迹上拟合门控的效果差异。

Result: 固定先验深度监督可产生难度感知的轨迹，其中间状态暴露出有用的停止信号；简单后验置信度读出常匹配或超越学习型线性门控和MLP门控；在冻结轨迹上拟合门控定位了失败来源：主要来自联合门控训练诱导的轨迹而非门控表达能力有限；预训练的ponder门控在Ouro评估中具有竞争力但非普遍Pareto最优。

Conclusion: 循环Transformer中的自适应深度应被视为轨迹形成和出口读出的联合问题，而非单是门控学习问题，这一区分在先前学习式停顿时机研究中常被隐含地忽略。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase test-time computation by repeatedly applying a shared recurrent block. Learned halting objectives in looped Transformers typically use a single exit distribution both as the inference-time stopping rule and as the training-time weighting of per-depth losses. This entangles exit selection with trajectory formation: the gate not only chooses which recurrent state to use, but also determines how strongly each intermediate state is supervised. Consequently, poor adaptive-compute performance can arise from the readout, the induced trajectory, or their interaction. We study adaptive depth in looped Transformers through this trajectory--readout lens, across controlled synthetic tasks (modular arithmetic and binary parity) and large-scale Ouro-1.4B and 2.6B checkpoints. We find that fixed-prior depth supervision, which shapes the trajectory without an input-dependent halting policy, produces difficulty-aware trajectories whose intermediate states expose useful stopping signals, and that simple post-hoc confidence readouts often match or outperform learned linear and MLP gates. Fitting gates on frozen trajectories localizes the failure: it appears to stem mainly from the trajectory induced by joint gate training rather than from limited gate expressivity. The same pattern is present in Ouro evaluations, where pretrained ponder gates are competitive but not uniformly Pareto-optimal, and measured latency confirms that the resulting reductions in average exit depth translate into practical inference-time savings. Our systematic diagnostic evaluation reframes adaptive depth in looped Transformers as a joint problem of trajectory formation and exit readout, rather than gate learning alone, highlighting a distinction that prior learned-halting work has often left implicit.

</details>


### [207] [Generative Bayesian Filtering for State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20521)
*Lei Cao,Sihang Feng,Jixin Yan,Tao Sun,Naichen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成贝叶斯滤波（GBF），用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）替代传统线性高斯观测模型，将滤波问题转化为基于分数的采样问题，在合成数据和真实制造业监控、心律不齐诊断任务中提升了状态估计精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统滤波方法（如卡尔曼滤波）依赖线性高斯等简单观测模型，无法刻画高维传感器信号中日益非线性和异质的模式。

Method: 提出GBF框架：用预训练的条件变分自编码器（CVAE）作为观测模型；将测量更新表述为后验采样问题，融合动力学先验与CVAE诱导的似然；将滤波问题转化为基于分数的采样问题。

Result: 在合成数据和真实应用（制造系统监控、心律不齐诊断）上，GBF相较基线方法在状态估计精度和鲁棒性上均有提升。

Conclusion: GBF兼具生成模型的灵活性与集成的不确定性量化能力，为复杂动态系统的高维非线性状态估计提供了一种有效框架。

Abstract: The state of a dynamic system evolves over time, switching among several latent modes that govern its observable behavior. Filtering methods infer the latent state from observations. Classical filtering approaches, including Kalman filters, typically rely on simple observation models, such as linear-Gaussian models, that are incapable of characterizing the increasingly nonlinear and heterogeneous patterns in high-dimensional sensor signals. To tackle the challenge, we propose Generative Bayesian Filtering (GBF), a filtering framework that replaces restrictive observation models with pretrained conditional generative models parametrized by conditional variational autoencoders (CVAE). For online inference, GBF performs a Bayesian prediction-update recursion in which the measurement update is formulated as a posterior sampling problem that combines the dynamical prior with the CVAE-induced likelihood. The resulting filtering problem is then transformed into a score-based sampling problem, which naturally inherits the flexibility from generative models and the uncertainty quantification capabilities from ensembling. Experiments on synthetic datasets and real-world applications involving manufacturing system monitoring and arrhythmia diagnosis demonstrate that GBF improves state estimation accuracy and robustness relative to baseline approaches.

</details>


### [208] [Do Active SAE Feature Planes Carry More Holonomy? A Preregistered Reversal in Gemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20522)
*Larry Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了Gemma 2 2B模型中holonomy是否集中在主动稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征平面上，结果发现该预测被反向证伪：主动特征平面的holonomy低于混合特征控制组，调整后的对数对比度为-0.29439，95%置信区间为[-0.43989, -0.14889]。纯幅度解释不成立，三向排序因共同支持缺失而无法定义。事后诊断支持面积律和有界匹配中心位移，并识别出传输畸变为潜在机制或混淆因素。结果仅为狭窄的、可审计的操作性反转，而非因果性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证语义集中假设（semantic-concentration prediction）的具体操作化版本——即holonomy是否集中在主动SAE特征平面上，从而检验语言模型内部表示结构的几何性质。

Method: 在Gemma 2 2B模型的最终token层，从layer-12到layer-13的残差流读出点测量holonomy：通过受限雅可比传输规则在局部环路上携带局部坐标系，将产生的旋转按包围面积归一化。实验设计、实质阈值、分析规则和判定规则均预先注册并在检查测量结果前冻结。

Result: 主动特征平面的holonomy显著低于匹配的混合特征控制组，调整后对数对比度为-0.29439，95%区间[-0.43989, -0.14889]。纯幅度解释不成立；三向排序因共同支持缺失而无法定义。事后诊断在验证子集上支持面积律，识别出匹配中心位移和传输畸变。

Conclusion: 该预测被反向证伪，但结果仅为狭窄的、可审计的操作性反转，不能得出“意义抑制holonomy”的因果结论。原因未被确定，激活强度几何、特征参与度、字典几何、匹配中心位移、激活流形邻近性和传输剪切均为可能解释。

Abstract: This paper tests whether holonomy concentrates on active sparse-autoencoder (SAE) feature planes in Gemma 2 2B, a concrete operationalization of the broader semantic-concentration prediction. Holonomy is measured at the final-token layer-12 to layer-13 residual-stream readout by carrying a local frame around small loops using the instrument's restricted-Jacobian transport rule, then normalizing the resulting rotation by enclosed area. The design, materiality threshold, analysis, and verdict rules were preregistered and frozen before the analysed measurements were inspected. The prediction was falsified in reverse: active-feature planes carried less holonomy than matched mixed-feature controls, with an adjusted log contrast of -0.29439 and 95% interval [-0.43989, -0.14889]. A magnitude-only explanation was not supported in this design, while the three-way ordering across random, mixed-feature, and active-feature planes was undefined at matched magnitude because common support failed. Post-freeze diagnostics at the same readout supported the area law on a small validation subset, bounded matched-center displacement under a simple paired regression, and identified transport distortion as a live mechanism or confound. The result is therefore a narrow, auditable operational reversal, not a causal claim that meaning suppresses holonomy. The cause remains open, with activation-strength geometry, degree of feature engagement, dictionary geometry, matched-center displacement, activation-manifold proximity, and transport shear as live alternatives.

</details>


### [209] [Uncertainty-Aware Trust Estimation for Multi-LLM Systems via Structured Expert Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20529)
*Jiawei Zheng,Jiazhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多LLM聚合问题建模为不确定性感知的信任估计，采用Cooke风格对数加权法，根据校准问题评估专家可靠性。在MMLU和MMLU-Pro上的实验表明，该方法在异质性和污染场景下优于传统聚合方法，实现了准确性-可靠性的更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM集成方法假设所有模型同样可信，忽略了不同LLM在不确定性质量和可靠性上的显著差异，导致聚合结果易受不可靠或对抗性专家影响。

Method: 将多LLM聚合形式化为不确定性感知的信任估计问题，借鉴决策理论中的结构化专家判断方法，使用上下文感知的校准问题评估专家概率预测质量，采用Cooke风格对数加权，惩罚过度自信的错误预测并奖励校准良好的专家。

Result: 在同质化场景下各方法表现相似；在异质性和污染场景下，Cooke加权方法在准确性-可靠性平衡上表现更优，对不可靠专家的引入具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多LLM聚合不仅需要组合预测，还需要在不确性下校准信任度。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) ensembles are increasingly used to improve reliability by combining predictions from multiple LLMs. However, existing aggregation methods typically assume that all models are equally trustworthy, overlooking differences in uncertainty quality. This assumption is poorly suited to heterogeneous LLMs, whose reliability and capability vary significantly, making naive aggregation vulnerable to unreliable or adversarial experts. In this work, we formulate multi-LLM aggregation as a problem of uncertainty-aware trust estimation. We adapt structured expert judgment from decision theory, using context-aware calibration questions to estimate expert reliability based on the quality of its probabilistic predictions. Specifically, we employ Cooke-style log weighting, which penalises overconfident incorrect predictions and favours well-calibrated experts. We evaluate our approach on MMLU and MMLU-Pro across homogeneous, heterogeneous, and contaminated expert panels. Results show that while aggregation methods perform similarly in homogeneous settings, Cooke weighting becomes critical under heterogeneity and contamination. It achieves a superior accuracy-reliability balance and remains robust when unreliable experts are introduced. These findings suggest that Multi-LLM aggregation requires not just combining predictions, but calibrating trust under uncertainty.

</details>


### [210] [Spectral Transformation for Layer-wise Global Rank Discovery in Federated LoRA for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21074)
*Hariharan Ramesh,Jyotikrishna Dass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecTraL，一种基于谱变换的联邦学习中Vision Transformer低秩适配器全局秩发现方法。通过正交Householder变换直接在低秩潜空间堆叠局部LoRA模块，利用随机矩阵理论的Spiked Covariance Model从非独立同分布噪声中分离全局共识信号，自动发现最优逐层全局秩，无需人工调参和密集权重重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LoRA聚合策略存在根本性局限：独立平均LoRA因子引入跨项聚合误差；保留异构客户端秩的拼接方法增加下载开销并导致重初始化滞后；重建密集权重更新或训练辅助模型则增加服务器端计算负担。亟需一种统一设计来消除这些缺陷。

Method: SpecTraL首先在服务器端堆叠各客户端的局部LoRA模块，然后对堆叠后的适配器直接执行正交Householder变换，所有操作均在低秩潜空间完成，避免全局更新的密集重建。基于随机矩阵理论中的Spiked Covariance Model，从非独立同分布噪声中解析分离全局共识信号，自动确定最优的逐层全局秩。为匹配后续轮次中的局部秩，引入填充感知初始化框架，使客户端无需将残差LoRA维度重新合并回预训练基座模型。

Result: 在DomainNet和NICO++数据集上对ViT-B/16和ViT-L/16进行联邦微调实验，结果表明SpecTraL在准确率-通信权衡、服务器计算开销减少以及消除秩选择的超参数搜索方面均取得改进。

Conclusion: SpecTraL通过谱变换与随机矩阵理论结合，以统一框架解决了联邦LoRA聚合中的多个关键问题，实现了更优的通信效率和计算效率，同时避免了繁琐的超参数调优。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Vision Transformers (ViTs) with low-rank adapters (LoRA) promises better communication efficiency under federated setup, yet existing aggregation strategies face fundamental limitations. Independently averaging these LoRA factors is mathematically inconsistent, introducing cross-term aggregation error. In contrast, approaches that preserve heterogeneous client ranks by concatenating local adapters on the server substantially increase download cost and often require merging global LoRA updates into pretrained weights on the clients, causing reinitialization lag and unstable convergence. Other approaches further increase server-side overhead by reconstructing dense weight updates or training auxiliary models to refine aggregation error. In this work, we propose SpecTraL, spectral transformation for layer-wise global rank discovery, that resolves these challenges within a unified design. SpecTraL stacks local LoRA modules from clients and performs orthonormal Householder Transformation of the stacked adapters directly in the low-rank latent space, eliminating dense reconstruction of the global update and any auxiliary refinement on the server. By leveraging the Spiked Covariance Model from Random Matrix Theory, SpecTraL analytically separates the global consensus signal from non-IID noise, discovering optimal layer-wise global ranks without manual hyperparameter tuning. To match local ranks in subsequent rounds, we introduce a padding-aware initialization framework that lets clients incorporate residual LoRA dimensions without re-merging them into the pre-trained base model. Experiments on federated fine-tuning of ViT-B/16 and ViT-L/16 over DomainNet and NICO++ demonstrate improved accuracy-communication trade-offs, reduced server computation, and elimination of hyperparameter search for rank selection. Our code is available at https://github.com/DASS-Lab-Group/SpecTraL

</details>


### [211] [CLOE: Christoffel Loss Autoencoder for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20530)
*Léa Billet,Louise Travé-Massuyès,Elodie Chanthery,Alexandre Gaffet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLOE方法，将自编码器与Christoffel函数结合，通过新型损失函数引导自编码器学习更优的潜在表示，实现高维表格数据的半监督异常检测，在多个基准上取得优异性能，同时保持了轻量级和低超参数调优的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级异常检测方法处理高维数据时表现不佳，且通常需要精细调优多个超参数。Christoffel函数方法虽然简单且理论基础扎实，但难以扩展到高维场景。

Method: CLOE将自编码器（用于降维）与Christoffel函数检测器（应用于潜在空间）相结合。设计了一种利用Christoffel函数引导自编码器的新损失函数，使表示学习更好地捕捉正常数据分布的支撑集。此外，提出了基于原理的检测阈值设定方法和高效的单超参数调优策略。

Result: 在多个高维表格异常检测基准上，CLOE相比现有方法取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: CLOE在保持Christoffel函数方法轻量级和低调优优势的同时，有效解决了其在高维数据上的可扩展性问题，为高维半监督异常检测提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Semi-supervised anomaly detection plays a key role in diverse fields such as process monitoring, healthcare, and finance. However, lightweight methods often struggle with high-dimensional data and typically require careful tuning of multiple hyperparameters. Among existing approaches, Christoffel Function--based methods are attractive due to their simplicity, requiring at most a single hyperparameter. They also benefit from a well-established theoretical foundation that yields several interesting results for data science. However, their main limitation is poor scalability to high-dimensional settings. In this paper, we introduce CLOE, a new method that combines an autoencoder for dimensionality reduction with a Christoffel Function--based detector applied in the latent space. To better align representation learning with anomaly detection, we design a novel loss function that leverages the Christoffel Function to guide the autoencoder toward representations that better capture the support of the normal data distribution. We further propose a principled procedure to set the detection threshold and an efficient strategy to tune the single remaining hyperparameter. Experiments on multiple high-dimensional tabular anomaly detection benchmarks demonstrate that CLOE achieves superior performance compared to existing methods, while preserving the lightweight and low-tuning advantages of Christoffel Function--based approaches.

</details>


### [212] [Position: Stop Reactively Patching Your Model Every Time and Start Proactive Test-Driven AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20532)
*Nadine Chang,Maying Shen,Jialiang Wang,Rafid Mahmood,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从反应式AI飞轮（仅依赖用户反馈修补错误）转向主动式测试驱动飞轮，通过构建“测试空间”将反馈数据映射到任务目标，以实现更好的长期可扩展性和更少的迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式AI飞轮存在两大局限：忽略错误的系统级上下文导致无法预判未来边缘案例，以及开放世界长尾分布下收集剩余错误的统计难度递增。

Method: 提出构建“测试空间”（test space）将用户反馈数据系统性地映射到任务目标，从而将飞轮从被动响应转变为主动预防。

Result: 通过数学证明，主动式飞轮相比反应式飞轮在长期扩展性上表现更优，且需要的迭代次数更少。

Conclusion: 主动式测试驱动飞轮是克服反应式飞轮局限性、迈向可泛化AI系统的必要路径。

Abstract: Many modern AI systems are designed to operate under diverse, open-ended, use-cases. To help generalize deployed systems, many deployed-system maintenance pipelines use a reactive AI flywheel that observes emerging feedback from user behavior (errors) and patches the model accordingly. However, when used as the primary maintenance mechanism, these flywheels often ignore the broader context of these errors within the system's objectives, failing to preempt potential future edge cases, which leads to more unnecessary flywheel iterations. Also, it is statistically increasingly difficult to collect remaining errors due to the long-tail nature of open-world use-cases. This position paper argues that a proactive test-driven flywheel is required to address reactive flywheel's limitations and to approach a generalizable system. We advocate for creating a "test space" to technically map feedback data to task objectives, evolving the flywheel from reactive to proactive. We augment our position by mathematically proving a proactive one achieves better long-term scaling with fewer iterations than the reactive flywheel.

</details>


### [213] [Grounding Investor Views: Neural Predicates in the Black-Litterman Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20533)
*Marcos Florencio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出使用神经谓词（Neural Predicates）作为结构化、概率化机制，为Black-Litterman模型自动生成投资观点及其置信度，实现从金融数据到投资组合权重的端到端可微学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Black-Litterman模型中投资者需要主观指定资产回报观点及其不确定性估计，这一过程主观性强且难以规模化，缺乏系统化的观点生成方法。

Method: 构建神经谓词的分层组合结构，处理结构化金融分析数据，输出市场立场的概率分布；将这些分布映射到Black-Litterman模型的P矩阵（观点矩阵）、q向量（观点回报向量）和Ω矩阵（观点不确定性矩阵）；观点置信度直接从谓词输出分布中导出。

Result: 该方法具有可解释性（任何组合权重可沿谓词逻辑链追溯到原始数据）和完全可微性（支持端到端学习），用数据驱动的方式替代了传统的主观不确定性 elicitation。

Conclusion: 神经谓词为Black-Litterman模型提供了一种结构化、可扩展、可解释且可端到端训练的观点生成框架，克服了传统方法主观性强和难以规模化的局限。

Abstract: Portfolio construction under the Black-Litterman model requires investors to specify views on asset returns alongside explicit uncertainty estimates -- a process that remains largely subjective and difficult to scale. We propose a formal approach in which neural predicates serve as a structured, probabilistic mechanism for view generation. In our formulation, structured financial analysis data is processed through a compositional hierarchy of neural predicates whose outputs -- probability distributions over market stances -- are mapped to the pick matrix $\mathbf{P}$, the view return vector $\mathbf{q}$, and the view uncertainty matrix $\boldsymbolΩ$ of the Black-Litterman model. View confidence is derived from predicate output distributions, providing a data-driven alternative to subjective uncertainty elicitation. The resulting approach is interpretable, in the sense that any portfolio weight can be traced back through the predicate's logical chain to the underlying data, and fully differentiable, enabling end-to-end learning.

</details>


### [214] [A Graph Neural Network approach to zero-shot Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20535)
*Alicia Tierz,Icíar Alfaro,David González,Elías Cueto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出零样本数字孪生框架，通过热力学信息图神经网络（TIGNN）与实时视觉感知耦合，实现无需重新训练即可在不同几何形状和物理场景（粘弹性梁大变形、粘性流体非线性晃荡）上进行物理仿真，并支持增强现实投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测性数字孪生在物理域或边界条件改变时需要大量重新训练或微调，缺乏几何泛化能力。

Method: (1) 热力学信息图神经网络（TIGNN）作为几何无关的物理推理引擎，基于度量-热力学形式约束能量守恒和熵产生；(2) 辅助GNN从稀疏视觉边界推断不可观测场（应力、速度、能量分布）；(3) 闭环数据同化：深度学习分割网络+稀疏光流实时跟踪宏观变形和自由表面，动态校正自回归仿真，消除数值漂移。

Result: 在粘弹性梁大变形和粘性流体非线性晃荡两个完全不同物理场景上，统一框架无需案例特定重新训练即可在新几何上生成物理精确仿真，每帧延迟约25ms，满足实时要求。

Conclusion: 零样本数字孪生框架成功实现了几何无关的实时物理仿真，弥合了仿真到现实的差距，并可通过增强现实直接投影潜在力学变量。

Abstract: Traditional Predictive Digital Twins often remain geometrically rigid, requiring extensive retraining or fine-tuning whenever the underlying physical domain or boundary conditions change. To overcome this limitation, we present a novel framework for \textit{Zero-Shot Digital Twins} that seamlessly couples real-time visual perception with a geometry-agnostic, physics-informed reasoning engine. At the core of our architecture is the Thermodynamics-Informed Graph Neural Network architecture, a Geometric Deep Learning solver grounded in a metriplectic thermodynamic formalism that enforces energy conservation and non-negative entropy production locally through graph message passing. The framework integrates an auxiliary Graph Neural Network to infer unobservable fields (such as stress tensors or velocity and energy distributions) directly from sparse initial visual boundaries, mitigating numerical start-up transients. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we implement a continuous closed-loop data assimilation mechanism; the pipeline tracks macroscopic deformations and free-surface fluid boundaries in real-time using deep segmentation networks combined with sparse optical flow, dynamically correcting the autoregressive simulation rollout and eliminating numerical drift. To test the validity of our approach, we demonstrate the extreme generalization capabilities of our approach across two disparate physical regimes: the large deformations of a viscoelastic beam and the non-linear sloshing of a viscous fluid. In both scenarios, the unified framework instantiates physically accurate simulations on novel, unseen geometries without case-specific retraining, operating well within real-time latency budgets (approximately 25 ms per frame) and enabling the direct projection of latent mechanical variables via Augmented Reality.

</details>


### [215] [ReliableTableQA:How Much Supervision Does Reliability Annotation Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20537)
*Huei-Chung Hu,Hsin-Tai Wu,Koyo Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReliableTableQA框架，通过十类可靠性分类体系(R1-R10)和程序优先数据流水线，用200个SFT样本即可将可靠性标注F1从0.61提升至0.98、解析率从0.52提升至1.00、UCAR降至零；GRPO仅在SFT欠训练时有效(+0.06-0.16)，SFT充分后无额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格QA系统即使SQL语法正确，也可能返回基于样本量过小、置信区间过宽或混杂因素过多的统计不可靠答案，但现有系统对所有情况都自信回答，缺乏对结果统计可靠性的标注能力。

Method: (1) 提出R1-R10十类可靠性分类体系；(2) 基于上下文无关语法构建程序优先数据流水线，自动生成50,000条可靠性标注训练样本；(3) 采用分层SFT和GRPO的对比训练方案，系统研究监督信号需求量。

Result: 200个SFT样本即可将可靠性标注F1从0.61提升至0.98、解析率从0.52提升至1.00、UCAR降至零，在未见过的H&M零售域上Rel-F1达0.997；GRPO仅在SFT欠训练时带来+0.06-0.16提升，SFT充分后无显著收益。

Conclusion: 可靠性标注本质上是一个数据效率问题，只需少量标注数据即可高效解决；RL微调(GRPO)的收益存在明确边界，仅在SFT数据不足时有价值，充足SFT后不再需要。
