Reproducible preprocessing is achieved through configurable pipelines defined in portable YAML configuration files, ensuring consistent data handling without distributing sensitive preprocessed data. Beyond preprocessing, GlucoTune provides a unified interface for implementing, training, and evaluating blood glucose prediction models. The framework integrates public datasets through standardized wrappers and provides a curated collection of state-of-the-art blood glucose prediction and general time-series forecasting methods, while remaining readily extensible to additional datasets, preprocessing strategies, and forecasting models. To promote transparent and consistent evaluation, GlucoTune includes a benchmarking leaderboard that reports results across datasets, preprocessing configurations, and forecasting methods, enabling systematic comparison of experimental settings. We demonstrate the effectiveness of GlucoTune through comprehensive experiments and assess its usability in a user study.

</details>


### [285] [Relative Value Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21120)
*Marc Höftmann,Jan Robine,Stefan Harmeling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出相对价值学习(RV)框架，通过反对称函数直接学习价值差异Δ(s_i,s_j)=V(s_i)-V(s_j)，替代传统的绝对状态价值估计，并在Atari基准上取得与PPO相当的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中，传统critic估计绝对状态价值V(s)，但实际控制中只有价值差异才是相关的，因此需要一种直接学习价值差异的方法。

Method: 提出RV框架：引入成对贝尔曼算子并证明其为γ-压缩映射且不动点为真实价值差异；推导1步、n步和λ-return目标；从成对差异重构广义优势估计，得到无偏策略梯度估计器R-GAE；将RV与PPO集成。

Result: 在49个ALE游戏的Atari基准上，RV-PPO与标准PPO相比取得了竞争性能，表明相对价值估计是绝对critic的有效替代方案。

Conclusion: 相对价值学习框架能够有效替代传统绝对价值估计方法，在强化学习中具有理论和实践价值。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning, critics typically estimate absolute state values $V(s)$, estimating how good a particular situation is in isolation. However, it turns out that only differences in value are relevant for control. Motivated by this, we propose Relative Value Learning (RV), a framework that learns value differences directly via an antisymmetric function $Δ(s_i, s_j) = V(s_i) - V(s_j)$. We introduce a pairwise Bellman operator and prove it is a $γ$-contraction with a unique fixed point equal to the true value differences, derive well-posed $1$-step, $n$-step and $λ$-return targets and reconstruct generalized advantage estimation from pairwise differences to obtain an unbiased policy-gradient estimator (R-GAE). Beyond theoretical results, we integrate RV with PPO and achieve competitive performance on the Atari benchmark (49 ALE games) compared to standard PPO, indicating that relative value estimation is an effective alternative to absolute critics.

</details>


### [286] [Demographically-Informed Heat-Mortality Risk Curves via Risk Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21131)
*Alex O. Davies,Eunice Lo,Rui Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出风险图神经网络（RGNN），利用细粒度人口普查特征优化DLNM系数向量，在保持可解释风险曲线的同时显著提升预测校准能力，在2022年热浪中基线模型失效时仍保持低点误差和近名义不确定性覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DLNM虽有效但忽略了与热脆弱性密切相关的人口统计和地理背景信息，导致预测校准不足。

Method: 提出风险图神经网络（RGNN），一种层次化GNN编码器，利用细粒度人口普查特征优化DLNM系数向量，保留可解释风险曲线输出。

Result: 在英格兰和威尔士10个地区两个前所未有的炎热年份评估中，RGNN变体在2022年热浪期间基线模型失效时仍保持较低的点误差和接近名义的不确定性覆盖。

Conclusion: RGNN能有效整合人口统计和地理上下文信息，在极端热浪事件中显著提升热相关死亡风险预测的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Estimating heat-related mortality risk is a core task in environmental epidemiology, typically addressed with Distributed Lag Non-linear Models (DLNMs); interpretable exposure-response surfaces fitted to temperature-mortality time series. DLNMs are effective but ignore demographic and geographic context, despite well-established relevance to heat vulnerability. We propose Risk Graph Neural Networks (RGNNs), a hierarchical GNN encoder that uses granular census features to optimise DLNM coefficient vectors, preserving interpretable risk curve outputs while substantially improving predictive calibration. Evaluated across 10 regions of England and Wales on two unprecedented heat years, RGNN variants maintain both lower point-errors and near-nominal uncertainty coverage during the 2022 heatwave where baselines collapse.

</details>


### [287] [Agree on the Model, Verify the Inference: GKR Protocols for HND-Based Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21162)
*Xiaolong Liang,Juanjuan Li,Rui Qin,Yisheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GKR-HND协议，用于验证同态-非同态分解Transformer的推理完整性，通过保留验证者检查GKR证明和注册权重，并将公开评估委托给计算工人，在诚实假设下确保安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外包Transformer推理存在模型替换和不完全执行的安全风险，而直接重放验证会消除委托计算的计算优势，需要在安全性和效率间取得平衡。

Method: 设计GKR-HND注册模型协议，保留验证者检查GKR（Goldwasser-Kalai-Rothblum）证明抄本和注册权重的公开值，将昂贵的公开评估委托给指定的计算工人，利用签名和请求绑定的响应确保一致性。

Result: 在预训练的HND（Homomorphic-Nonhomomorphic Decomposition）模型上实验，验证了证明路径和委托公开计算的有效性，避免了稠密矩阵重放的开销。

Conclusion: GKR-HND在诚实保留验证者和证明-工人非共谋假设下，能确保仅当工人的签名绑定响应与证明声明一致时验证者才接受，为Transformer推理完整性验证提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Outsourced Transformer inference exposes clients to model substitution and incomplete execution, while direct replay removes the computational benefit of delegation. We present GKR-HND, a registered-model protocol for verifying the polynomial backbone of Homomorphic--Nonhomomorphic Decomposition Transformers. The retained verifier checks the GKR transcript and registered-weight openings, but delegates expensive public evaluations to an assigned computation worker. Assuming an honest retained verifier and prover--worker non-collusion, the verifier accepts only when the worker's signed, request-bound response agrees with the proof claims. Experiments with pretrained HND models validate the proof path and the delegated public computation without dense-matrix replay.

</details>


### [288] [Automated Synthesis and Adversarial Validation of Executable Causal Research Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21173)
*Irena Girshovitz,Dan Zeltzer,Ran Gilad-Bachrach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARA是一个基于AI的流行病学研究助手框架，通过显式编码因果设计原则、协议构建、合成数据生成和对抗性验证，将自然语言研究问题转化为结构化因果协议和可执行代码。虽然该方法在数值精度上未显著优于标准LLM生成，但改变了失败模式——从静默返回因果估计转变为主动暴露协议问题、诊断失败或不完整推理，从而使得无效因果假设变得可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究系统虽然有望加速实证分析，但容易出现静默失败（silent failures），即分析代码成功执行但依赖无效的因果假设。现有方法无法使这些失败可见，因此需要一种能够显式暴露因果假设有效性的框架。

Method: ARA框架集成了三个核心组件：1) 协议构建：将自然语言研究问题转化为结构化因果协议；2) 合成数据生成：使用结构因果模型（SCMs）生成具有已知真实效应的合成数据集，支持在受限数据环境下开发；3) 对抗性验证：在违反识别假设的条件下评估生成的分析结果。整个流程形成一个统一的流水线。

Result: 在自动因果推理基准上评估显示，ARA在识别策略、因果量、处理变量和结果变量的恢复以及代码与协议的一致性方面，协议构建和对抗性验证并未持续提高与基准估计的数值一致性。但ARA改变了失败模式：从静默返回因果估计，转变为主动暴露协议问题、诊断失败、不完整推理或降级为非因果解释。

Conclusion: 有效性优先（validity-first）的自动化科学系统的评估不应仅依据答案准确性，还应考察系统在因果主张不合理时是否能够发出警示信号。

Abstract: While automated research systems promise to accelerate empirical analysis, they are prone to silent failures: instances in which analysis code executes successfully yet relies on invalid causal assumptions. We present the Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based Epidemiology Research Assistant (ARA), a framework that makes these failures visible by explicitly encoding causal design principles, study-specific assumptions, and methodological constraints. ARA integrates protocol construction, synthetic data generation, and adversarial validation into a unified pipeline. The framework translates natural language research questions into structured causal protocols and executable analysis code by first constructing a protocol and then generating synthetic datasets using Structural Causal Models (SCMs) with known ground-truth effects. This synthetic-data step can also support pipeline development when access to confidential data, such as medical data, is restricted. The generated analysis is then evaluated under controlled violations of identification assumptions. We evaluate ARA on the Automated Causal Reasoning Benchmark, assessing recovery of identification strategies, causal quantities, treatment and outcome variables, and consistency between generated code and approved protocol. Protocol construction and adversarial validation did not consistently improve numerical agreement with benchmark estimates compared with standard LLM-based generation. However, they changed the failure mode: instead of silently returning causal estimates, ARA often surfaced protocol concerns, diagnostic failures, incomplete inference, or downgraded non-causal interpretations. These findings suggest that validity-first automated science systems should be evaluated not only by answer accuracy, but also by whether they indicate when causal claims are unwarranted.

</details>


### [289] [Filter Learning for Subgraphs: Algebras and Performance Risk Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21263)
*Purui Zhang,Feng Ji,Yanan Zhao,Bihan Wen,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种子图滤波器学习（SFL）框架，利用基于距离感知拉普拉斯构造的子图滤波器代数，在仅观测部分图拓扑的情况下近似全局图滤波器，并在理论上建立了风险界，实验证明该方法优于多项式滤波器、分布无关算子和直接数值学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图信号处理中的谱方法通常假设完整的图拓扑信息可用，但实际中往往只能观测到部分图结构。现有方法无法有效利用子图上的局部信息来近似全局滤波器，因此需要系统性地学习子图支持的算子。

Method: 将子图滤波器学习形式化为统计学习问题；提出基于距离感知拉普拉斯构造的子图滤波器代数，定义结构化的可控滤波器类；在最小二乘损失下建立性能风险界，量化学习算子对受限全局映射的逼近程度。

Result: 在真实数据集上，所提出的代数模型在子图滤波器学习任务中持续优于多项式滤波器、分布无关算子以及直接数值滤波器学习基线。

Conclusion: 子图滤波器代数提供了一种结构化且可控的方式在局部观测下近似全局谱算子，理论风险界保证了其逼近能力，实验验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Graph signal processing tasks that leverage spectral information typically assume access to the complete graph topology, which is often unavailable in practice. We propose a systematic framework for subgraph filter learning (SFL), where subgraph-supported operators approximate ambient graph filters under partial observations. We formulate SFL as a statistical learning problem in which optimal subgraph operators are inherently data-dependent. To address the difficulty of directly estimating such operators, we develop a subgraph filter algebra based on distance-aware Laplacian constructions, defining a structured and controllable class of filters for effective approximation. We further establish performance risk bounds under the least squares loss, quantifying how well the learned operator approximates the restricted ambient mapping. Experiments real-world datasets show that, for SFL tasks, the proposed algebraic models consistently outperform polynomial filters, distribution-agnostic operators, and direct numerical filter learning baselines that attempt to recover the underlying structure from data.

</details>


### [290] [The Dark Room in the Reward Channel: Dense Prediction Rewards Collapse GRPO-Trained LLM Agents -- and What Actually Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21273)
*Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示在GRPO（分组归一化强化学习）下，基于预测的稠密奖励不仅无效，反而会破坏策略——导致模型陷入“黑暗房间”病理状态（预测准确率接近1，任务成功率为0）。问题根源在于GRPO的z-score归一化将稠密信号的组内方差放大，当所有组都失败时，归一化优势对奖励系数不变，使有界奖励变成无界压力。消除标准差归一化可恢复基线性能。进一步的信号通道实验表明，奖励通道最多中性，而辅助损失通道可提升约20个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为稀疏奖励、长程LLM智能体寻找有效的稠密逐步监督方法：通过奖励智能体预测其下一个观测，期望能自然习得记忆。但需要验证该方法的有效性及潜在问题。

Method: 在ALFWorld环境中使用Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B模型，采用基于势能的预测奖励进行GRPO训练。通过单因素消融实验定位问题，进行方差-分布准则分析，设计信号递送矩阵实验对比奖励通道与辅助损失通道，并包含乱序金标签安慰剂对照。

Result: 1）基于势能的预测奖励在GRPO下导致策略完全崩溃（任务成功率0%，预测准确率100%）；2）单因素消融表明去除GRPO的std归一化即可将灾难恢复至基线水平；3）提出方差-分布准则解释：z-score放大的是组内方差，当所有组都失败时，有界奖励被转化为无界压力；4）信号递送实验表明奖励通道最多中性，辅助损失通道提升约20点；5）乱序金标签安慰剂与真实金标签结果相当。

Conclusion: GRPO的z-score归一化与稠密预测奖励的结合存在根本性冲突，会诱导优化器自动构建“黑暗房间”病理状态。方差-分布准则可作为预测此类坍塌的判断依据。奖励通道不适合传递稠密信号，辅助损失通道是更优选择。

Abstract: Dense per-step supervision is an appealing remedy for sparse-reward, long-horizon LLM agents: reward the agent for predicting its next observation, and memory should follow. We show that under group-normalized RL (GRPO), this recipe does not merely fail -- it destroys the policy. Across Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B on ALFWorld, a potential-based prediction reward drives every run into a degenerate absorbing state (prediction accuracy -> 1.0, task success -> 0,episode length pinned at the horizon): the "dark room" pathology, built automatically by the optimizer. A single-factor ablation localizes the cause -- removing only GRPO's std normalization turns the same reward from catastrophic (0%) into baseline parity -- and a two-line proposition explains why: in all-fail groups the z-scored advantage is invariant to the shaping coefficient, so bounded rewards become unbounded pressure and annealing cannot help. Our central insight generalizes this: what z-scoring amplifies is a dense signal's within-group variance while all-fail groups dominate, so signals whose variance decays by mastery are structurally amplifier-safe.This variance-profile criterion retrodicts our collapses, carries preregistered predictions for arms that had not yet run, and is consistent with published reward-channel successes (a compatibility check, not an independent test). Finally, a controlled signal-delivery matrix (identical signal, varying only the consumption mechanism) shows the reward channel is at best neutral while the auxiliary-loss channel gains ~20 points -- and a shuffled-gold placebo matches the true-gold arm, so the gap survives without correct labels. Endpoints are single-seed; seed replication and group-size controls are preregistered and in progress.

</details>


### [291] [Multi-Task Learning for Heterogeneous Prediction from Video Game State with Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21290)
*Jonas Peché,Aliaksei Tsishurou,Alexander Zap,Günter Wallner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于多任务学习（MTL）的团队多人在线游戏状态预测方法，提出一种融合图像编码与注意力机制的多模态架构，在大型《坦克世界》数据集上实验证明共享模型相比单任务模型能提升泛化性能并降低训练推理成本，同时探讨了损失加权、梯度冲突处理、预训练微调及跨地图迁移等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代游戏遥测数据提供多个相关的监督信号，但传统单任务模型忽视了任务间共享信息，且训练推理成本高。MTL有望利用这些信号提升泛化性能并降低成本。

Method: 提出一种多模态多任务架构，融合栅格化视觉输入、全局比赛上下文和单位状态信息，通过图像编码器与基于注意力的交互建模进行端到端预测。实验包括单任务vs多任务对比、混合损失加权策略、冲突梯度处理、预训练微调及跨地图迁移测试。

Result: 在《坦克世界》大规模专有数据集上，多任务共享模型相比单任务模型在泛化性能上更优，同时降低了训练和推理成本。不同加权策略和梯度处理方法影响最终效果，预训练微调在目标数据有限时有效，跨地图迁移场景下模型表现出一定鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多任务学习在基于游戏遥测的状态预测任务中是一种有效且高效的范式，共享表示能提升泛化并节省资源，但仍需仔细处理多任务损失平衡和任务间冲突。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) is a promising approach for prediction tasks derived from video game state data, as modern game telemetry provides multiple related supervision signals from the same structured observations. We study whether a shared model trained jointly across tasks in team-based multiplayer games can improve generalization while reducing training and inference cost compared to specialized single-task models. We adapt a multimodal architecture for endpoint prediction to a general multi-task setting that combines rasterized vision inputs, global match context, and per-unit state information through an image encoder and attention-based interaction modeling. Experiments on a large proprietary World of Tanks dataset compare single-task and multi-task training, evaluate weighting strategies for mixed losses and conflicting gradients, and test pre-training/fine-tuning under limited target-data regimes. We also examine within-game transfer across game maps under structured environment shift.

</details>


### [292] [AI Assistants Overassist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21306)
*Verona Teo,Raghav Jain,Tobias Gerstenberg,Max Kleiman-Weiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Int-Bench，一个基于模拟的基准测试，用于评估LLM在学习过程中作为教师时的干预决策。研究发现，与人类相比，LLM教师干预更频繁、更早，且倾向于提供完整解决方案而非针对性提示，表明它们更优化短期成功而非支持深层学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作导师和思维伙伴，但过度或过早的干预可能抑制真正的学习和认知参与。目前尚不清楚AI系统在问题解决过程中如何做出干预决策，因此需要评估LLM的干预行为。

Method: 提出了Int-Bench，一个模拟学生解决问题的基准测试。该基准模拟"学生"解决问题，同时"教师"LLM监测学生的推理过程并决定是否、何时以及如何干预。在代码调试、数学和脑经急转弯三个领域评估LLM教师的干预频率、时机及其对即时任务成功和泛化的影响，并与人类进行对比。

Result: LLM教师的干预频率比人类更频繁、更早；与人类不同，LLM倾向于提供完整解决方案而非针对性提示。

Conclusion: 当前的LLM助手往往优化短期任务成功，而非支持深层学习所需的推理过程和长期成功。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as tutors and thought partners, helping users reason through problems. While guidance from AI assistants can scaffold thinking and foster learning, such benefits depend on how they help--for instance, intervening too early or too frequently may hinder true learning and cognitive engagement. Yet how AI systems navigate intervention decisions during problem-solving remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce Int-Bench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating LLM interventions during learning. Int-Bench simulates a "student" solving a problem while a "teacher" monitors the student's reasoning and decides whether, when, and how to intervene. Across three domains--code debugging, mathematics, and brain teasers--we evaluate LLM teachers on the frequency and timing of interventions, as well as their impact on both immediate task success and generalization to new problems. We also compare LLMs to humans, finding that LLMs intervene more frequently and earlier than humans. Moreover, in contrast to humans, they tend to provide complete solutions rather than targeted hints. These findings suggest that current LLM assistants often optimize for short-term success rather than supporting the reasoning processes needed for deeper learning and long-term success.

</details>


### [293] [M$^3$-Gen: Interpretable Multimodal Generation of Gene Expression Profiles Using Clinical and Imaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21343)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Carlo Sgaravatti,Marco Venere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M$^3$-Gen是一种新型生成式框架，通过对抗网络结合组织病理学图像和临床元数据生成基因表达谱，利用对比学习统一多模态表征，并具备内在可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据获取成本高、隐私限制大，限制了多模态研究和AI应用；需要一种能从易获取模态（图像+临床数据）生成基因表达的方法。

Method: 以条件生成对抗网络为核心，通过对比学习从临床变量和组织病理学图像中学习统一潜在表征，利用注意力机制融合多模态嵌入指导基因表达生成。

Result: 在TCGA数据集上验证了生成的真实性和功能意义；注意力机制可识别影响特定基因表达生成的组织病理图像区域，实现内在可解释性。

Conclusion: M$^3$-Gen能够从组织病理图像和临床元数据中生成生物上连贯的基因表达谱，解决了数据稀缺问题，且设计上具备可解释性。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous biomedical data, including clinical metadata, histopathology images, and molecular profiles, is crucial for comprehensive disease understanding. However, gene expression data acquisition remains constrained by high costs and privacy concerns, limiting its use in multimodal research and AI-driven applications. We present MultiModal Molecular Generation (M$^3$-Gen), a novel framework for the generation of gene expression profiles by conditioning a Generative Adversarial Network on histopathology images and clinical metadata. M$^3$-Gen learns a unified latent representation from the clinical variables and the images, leveraging contrastive learning, and exploits the embeddings of the two modalities to guide a generative model in producing biologically coherent gene expression profiles. Evaluations on the TCGA dataset demonstrate that M$^3$-Gen generates realistic and functionally meaningful gene expression data. Importantly, by integrating multiple modalities in an attention-based mechanism, M$^3$-Gen provides intrinsic explainability: it allows the identification of which regions of the histopathology images most strongly influenced the generation of specific gene expression profiles, making the model's decisions interpretable by design.

</details>


### [294] [How Many Bits Can an Adapter Write? Measuring the Capacity and Memorization of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21351)
*Kaizhen Tan,Heqing Du,Yang Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过压缩理论分析，直接测量了低秩适配器（LoRA）在冻结基础模型上写入的信息量（比特数），发现其容量远小于全参数微调，且存储容量不仅取决于参数数量，更取决于参数在架构中的位置。实验表明，适配器在MLP层的容量约为Attention层的两倍，且监督微调会完整复制训练数据中的秘密，而基于可验证奖励的强化学习则不会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA适配器通常被视为一种"技能"而非训练数据的记录，但这种假设缺乏定量验证。本文旨在将这一"民间传说"转化为可量化的指标，直接测量微调过程写入模型的信息量。

Method: 将基于压缩的记忆化分析方法扩展至冻结基础模型（frozen-base）设置，直接以比特为单位测量LoRA适配器向未改变的模型中写入了多少信息。

Result: 1. LoRA适配器每个可训练参数存储约几比特，远低于全模型预算；2. 容量取决于参数位置而非数量——相同参数预算移到MLP层比Attention层容量翻倍；3. 破坏冻结基础的结构后容量几乎消失；4. 隐私泄露与写入的比特数相关，而非名义参数数；5. 监督微调会完整复制秘密，基于可验证奖励的强化学习则不会。

Conclusion: 测量微调写入的信息量而非事后攻击，可以将民间传说转化为可进行对抗设计（design against）的量化指标，为隐私保护提供理论依据。

Abstract: A LoRA adapter is a few megabytes that almost everyone treats as a skill rather than a record of the data behind it. We put that assumption on a scale. Extending compression-based memorization analysis to the frozen-base setting, we measure directly, in bits, how much a low-rank adapter writes into a model it never changes. The answer is both smaller than full fine-tuning and less lawful than parameter counting would predict. Adapters store a couple of bits per trainable parameter, well short of a full model's budget, but that figure turns less on how many parameters an adapter carries than on where they sit. Move the same parameter budget from attention into the MLP and it holds nearly twice as much; strip the frozen base of its structure and the capacity all but disappears. Applied to realistic fine-tunes of Qwen2.5, the same instrument shows privacy leakage rising with the bits an adapter writes rather than the parameters it nominally has, and it draws a clean line between supervised and reinforcement learning: the secrets that supervised fine-tuning copies down verbatim, an adapter trained on verifiable rewards never records. Measuring what fine-tuning writes, rather than attacking it after the fact, turns a piece of folklore into a quantity one can design against.

</details>


### [295] [Gradient Concentration, Not Weight Saliency, Explains Representation-Level Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21353)
*Billel Habbati,Alessio Merlo,Luca Verderame,Meriem Guerar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控消融实验发现，基于显著性的权重选择机制（如SalUn）在表示层面遗忘效果与随机掩码或无约束更新无显著差异。遗忘梯度高度集中在最终网络层（约92%），且显著性掩码缺乏类别特异性（特异性指数0.09-0.11），表明表示层面遗忘主要由梯度集中性和表示几何结构驱动，而非掩码选择策略本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于梯度显著性的参数选择方法在机器遗忘中被广泛采用，但其对表示层面遗忘的实际贡献尚不明确。本文旨在通过第一个受控消融实验，严格评估显著性掩码机制在表示层面遗忘中的真实作用。

Method: 采用匹配计算实验设计，在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上使用ResNet-18，比较显著性掩码、等稀疏度随机掩码和无约束更新三种配置，保持遗忘目标、优化计划和计算预算固定。通过线性探针、原型恢复和逐层CKA等多种表示层面评估方法进行对比分析。

Result: 三种配置在表示层面可恢复性上统计等价；遗忘梯度中约92%的平方梯度能量集中在最终网络层；显著性掩码的类别特异性指数仅为0.09-0.11，不同遗忘类别间参数子集高度重叠。

Conclusion: 在研究的设定下，表示层面遗忘主要由梯度集中性和表示几何结构主导，而非显著性选择权重的具体身份。有效的表示层面遗忘需要直接作用于潜在表示的目标函数，而非日益复杂的权重选择策略。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data while preserving model utility. Many state-of-the-art approaches pursue this goal by restricting the forgetting update to a subset of parameters selected through gradient-based saliency. Although such methods are widely adopted, the actual contribution of saliency-based weight selection to representation-level forgetting remains unclear. In this work, we perform the first controlled ablation of the saliency masking mechanism used by SalUn. Using a matched-compute experimental design on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ResNet-18, we compare saliency-based masking against random masks of equal sparsity and unconstrained updates, while keeping the unlearning objective, optimization schedule, and computational budget fixed. Across multiple representation-level evaluations, including linear probing, prototype recovery, and layer-wise CKA, the three configurations exhibit statistically equivalent representation-level recoverability. We find that forget gradients are strongly concentrated in the final network layers (approximately 92% of the squared gradient energy on CIFAR-10) before any mask is applied, causing all masking strategies to operate within the same representational subspace. Furthermore, saliency masks show limited class specificity (specificity index 0.09-0.11), selecting highly overlapping parameter subsets across different forget classes. Our findings suggest that, in the studied setting, representation-level forgetting is primarily governed by gradient concentration and representation geometry rather than by the specific identity of saliency-selected weights. More broadly, the results support a growing body of evidence indicating that effective representation-level unlearning requires objectives that act directly on latent representations rather than on increasingly sophisticated weight-selection strategies.

</details>


### [296] [Emergent Misalignment Recruits a Pre-existing Persona Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21356)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在窄领域恶意数据上微调对齐的语言模型会引发广泛的"涌现性失调"，其根本原因是微调激活了模型中预先存在的"人格子空间"结构。从冻结的指令调优模型中提取出跨领域共享的低秩核心，该核心显著偏离随机子空间。将该子空间移除可完全阻止广泛失调，注入则可诱导失调，而权重编辑等后处理方法无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为什么在窄领域恶意数据上微调会导致模型在无关问题上出现广泛的错误对齐行为（涌现性失调），揭示其背后的机制。

Method: 使用对比教师强制法从冻结的Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct模型中提取每个领域的"人格子空间"；通过低秩分析发现跨领域共享核心；在微调过程中将子空间从残差流中投影去除以阻断失调；通过注入子空间诱导失调；对比权重梯度投影、后验权重编辑等干预措施。

Result: 4个无关领域共享一个低秩核心（显著性达随机子空间的657倍），其中82%位于风格核心之外；微调第一步即开始攀升广泛失调边界；投影去除子空间将失调率从27.7%降至0.0%；注入子空间可诱导失调至45.4%；权重编辑无法消除结构，结构会在编辑清除的子空间内重建；跨领域分散恶意数据比权重叠加与多样性共同解释的效应更强。

Conclusion: 窄领域恶意微调引发的广泛失调源于激活了模型中预先存在的人格子空间结构，而非纯粹从数据中学习。通过操纵该子空间可以控制失调行为，但传统权重编辑方法难以根除。

Abstract: Fine-tuning an aligned language model on a narrow stream of bad advice can make it broadly misaligned on questions unrelated to the training data, a phenomenon called emergent misalignment. We ask why the narrow lesson generalizes at all, and we find that narrow fine-tuning recruits a persona structure that is present in the model before the fine-tune exists. From a frozen instruction-tuned model (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct) we extract per-domain persona subspaces by contrastive teacher forcing and find that 4 unrelated domains share one low-rank core at 657x a random-subspace null, with 82% of that core lying outside a style core built at matched diversity. The literal first optimizer step of fine-tuning on insecure code climbs a broad-misalignment margin harder than the same code framed as educational, and forecasts realized margin movement out to 375 steps. Projecting the subspace out of the residual stream throughout fine-tuning prevents broad misalignment (27.7% to 0.0% of judged generations) while a matched-rank random subspace changes nothing; injecting it into the never-fine-tuned model induces misalignment that grows with dose to 45.4%, past the fine-tuned model it is measured against. The same projection applied to the weight gradient is inert, and three post-hoc weight edits leave the disposition in place: the sharpest edit suppresses the behavior rather than removing it, and the ablated structure re-forms inside the subspace the edit cleared. Spreading a fixed budget of bad data across 4 domains produces more broad misalignment than mechanical weight superposition and matched diversity jointly account for. All measurements come from one model at 14B; the extraction is from an aligned instruction-tuned checkpoint, which leaves the structure's provenance open; and the intervention that prevents misalignment also abolishes the narrow trained behavior.

</details>


### [297] [Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE): A Mathematical Framework for Deconstructing Learned Representations in Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21366)
*Hossein Mobahi,Peter L. Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HOPE（Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding）框架，通过将网络压缩从离散域迁移到连续函数的希尔伯特空间，将神经元建模为秩-1 Hilbert-Schmidt算子，统一了剪枝和神经元合并作为低维子空间投影，并引入宏块驱逐以处理多层结构。该框架无需数据和超参数，在模型压缩和微调中展示了实践潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络编码了复杂的内部表征，但解构这些内部知识仍然具有挑战性。学习与压缩之间存在关联，网络压缩为分析这些知识提供了有前景的视角，但标准压缩启发式方法常受尺度对称性和架构偏差的影响。

Method: HOPE将网络压缩从离散域迁移到连续函数的希尔伯特空间；将单个神经元建模为秩-1 Hilbert-Schmidt算子；将剪枝和神经元合并统一为低秩子空间投影；引入宏块驱逐以涵盖完整残差通路等多层结构。

Result: HOPE是一个无数据、无超参数的框架，能够在不同类型和大小的层间实现无偏的架构决策，并在模型压缩和微调的概念验证实验中展示了实际潜力。

Conclusion: HOPE通过希尔伯特空间中的数学框架，提供了一种统一、无偏的方式来解构和压缩深度神经网络中的内部表征，克服了传统方法中尺度对称性和架构偏差的局限。

Abstract: Deep neural networks encode complex representations, but deconstructing this internal knowledge remains a challenge. Given the link between learning and compression, network compression offers a promising lens to analyze this knowledge. However, standard compression heuristics often suffer from scale symmetries and architectural biases. To resolve these, we introduce Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE), a mathematical framework to gradually deconstruct the representations in trained network weights.
  HOPE shifts network compression from the discrete domain into a Hilbert space of continuous functions. By modeling individual neurons as rank-1 Hilbert-Schmidt operators, HOPE unifies pruning and neuron merging as low-rank subspace projection. Extending this formulation, HOPE introduces macro block eviction to encompass multi-layer structures like entire residual pathways under the same unified metric. This unified approach enables unbiased architectural decisions across layers with different types and sizes. HOPE is a data-free and hyperparameter-free framework. We present proof-of-concept experiments in model compression and fine-tuning to highlight the practical potential of our theory.

</details>


### [298] [Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21372)
*Jingyuan Li,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mean-to-Score (M2S)方法，通过预测干净token的后验均值并利用精确的核依赖线性映射转换为分数，解决了Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD)中分数比率违反贝叶斯可实现性的问题。M2S在CIFAR-10和OpenWebText上均显著优于SEDD及其他离散扩散基线，且无需改变采样器即可消除负权重并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEDD中未约束的分数比率虽然保证正性，但无法确保贝叶斯可实现性——即含噪状态下的分数比率不一定由任何干净token后验通过前向核诱导产生。这导致训练后的SEDD检查点中大量分数向量违反概率单纯形约束，产生负的预归一化权重，损害生成性能。

Method: 提出Mean-to-Score (M2S)方法：先预测干净token的后验均值，再通过精确的核依赖线性映射将其转换为分数比率。该构造适用于任何满足温和支持条件的坐标级连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）。对于均匀 corruption，该映射将概率单纯形映射到桥多面体；对于吸收掩码 corruption，目标函数精确恢复MD4。

Result: 在28.4M参数CIFAR-10上，M2S将测试BPD从3.173降至3.129，FID-50k显著降低。在170M参数OpenWebText上（约262B token训练），M2S在每步采样预算下均优于纯均匀SEDD、GIDD和Neural CTMC基线，128步时生成PPL达143.3，而最强纯均匀基线为183.6。投影原始分数到桥多面体可消除所有负权重，并将生成PPL从203.6改善至175.1。

Conclusion: M2S通过强制分数比率满足贝叶斯可实现性约束，有效提升了离散扩散模型的生成质量，是一种通用且高效的替代SEDD分数参数化的方法。

Abstract: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) parameterizes discrete reverse processes with unconstrained positive score ratios. While positivity guarantees nonnegative reverse jump rates, it does not ensure Bayes realizability: ratios at a noisy state need not be jointly induced by any clean-token posterior under the forward kernel. The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. In a trained pure-uniform SEDD checkpoint, roughly one quarter of complete score vectors violate the coordinate box, while more than half lie inside it yet remain materially incompatible with any valid posterior. Such violations can produce negative pre-normalization weights in finite-step sampling. Projecting raw scores onto the bridge polytope removes all observed negative weights and improves external generative PPL from $203.6$ to $175.1$ without changing the sampler. We introduce \emph{mean-to-score} (M2S), which predicts a clean-token posterior mean and converts it to the score through an exact kernel-dependent linear map. The construction applies to any known coordinate-wise continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) satisfying a mild support condition. For uniform corruption, it maps the probability simplex onto the bridge polytope; for absorbing-mask corruption, the resulting objective recovers MD4 exactly. In a controlled 28.4M-parameter CIFAR-10 comparison, M2S lowers test BPD from $3.173$ to $3.129$ and FID-50k from $\CifarSEDDFID$ to $\CifarMtwoSFID$. A 170M-parameter M2S model trained on about 262B OpenWebText token slots outperforms the evaluated pure-uniform SEDD, GIDD, and Neural CTMC checkpoints at every tested sampling budget, reaching generative PPL $143.3$ at 128 steps versus $183.6$ for the strongest pure-uniform baseline.

</details>


### [299] [A Diffusion-Model Subpopulation Digital Twin for Mobile Health Deployment: A Case Study on the HeartSteps Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21403)
*Ziping Xu,Yuyi Chang,Chenshun Ni,Nithin Sugavanam,Asim H. Gazi,Pedja Klasnja,Emre Ertin,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于条件时间序列扩散模型的“JITAI-Twins”数字孪生方法，用于在即时自适应干预部署前模拟目标子群体，通过三步更新（预训练、微调、推理时校准）生成逼真的用户行为序列，并在HeartSteps系列干预中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康干预中的在线学习算法若设计不当会加重用户负担并导致参与度下降，因此需要在实际部署前对候选算法进行真实模拟用户测试，但目前缺乏能够生成逼真用户行为序列的方法。

Method: 基于条件时间序列扩散模型构建数字孪生，确保时间一致性（未来动作不影响过去）；采用三步信息更新：1）在大规模观测数据上预训练，2）在相关人群的小规模先验干预数据上微调，3）利用领域专家知识在推理时校准到目标人群。在HeartSteps系列（v2到v4）中进行验证。

Result: 所提方法在再现目标子群体的时间结构和参与者间结构上优于更简单的模拟器，能够用于模拟即将部署的干预目标，为在线算法设计决策提供测试基础。

Conclusion: JITAI-Twins可以作为一种有效的预部署仿真工具，用于测试和指导移动健康干预中的在线算法设计决策，降低算法设计不当带来的风险。

Abstract: Mobile-health interventions increasingly use online learning and decision making algorithms to personalize when to nudge users toward healthier behavior, but a poorly designed algorithm can burden and disengage participants. New algorithm design decisions should therefore be vetted against realistic simulated users before each real-life deployment. We propose a method to develop ``JITAI-Twins'': digital twins of a target subpopulation for comparing candidate online algorithms before a just-in-time adaptive intervention (JITAI) deployment. The method builds on a conditional time-series diffusion model that is temporally consistent (future actions do not affect the generated past), and it supports repeated updating from three sources of information, in three steps: pre-training on a large observational dataset, fine-tuning on small prior intervention deployments in related populations, and inference-time calibration to the next target population from domain-scientist expertise. We validate the twin at each pre-deployment stage of the long-running HeartSteps series (v2 through v4) of physical-activity suggestion intervention deployments, treating each successive deployment as an upcoming study. The proposed method reproduces the target subpopulation's temporal and between-participant structure better than simpler simulators. These results suggest that our twin can be used to simulate a target deployment before it runs, the prerequisite for testing and informing online algorithm design decisions.

</details>


### [300] [Semantic-Aware Task Clustering for Constructive and Cooperative Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21426)
*Ahmad Halimi Razlighi,Maximilian H. V. Tillmann,Edgar Beck,Bho Matthiesen,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对协同多任务语义通信（CMT-SemCom）中任务间语义关系可能导致破坏性协作的问题，提出了一种基于语义感知的任务聚类方法。该方法通过分层密度空间聚类将语义对齐任务分组，随后仅在组内进行端到端联合训练，从而有效缓解负迁移，提升准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMT-SemCom通过共享表示提升任务执行性能，但任务间语义关系可能产生破坏性协作（负迁移），需要一种方法确保协作的建构性。

Method: 提出语义感知任务聚类方法，将问题分解为两阶段：①基于分层密度空间聚类（HDBSCAN）的语义聚类；②聚类内部端到端CMT-SemCom联合训练。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架有效缓解了破坏性协作和负迁移，相比未聚类多任务和单任务训练基线获得了精度提升。

Conclusion: 语义感知任务聚类能够确保CMT-SemCo中任务间建构性协作，提升系统整体性能。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-task semantic communication (CMT-SemCom) improves task execution performance by leveraging shared representations. However, as we demonstrated in [1], cooperative multi-tasking can be either constructive or destructive, depending on the semantic relationships among tasks. To ensure constructive cooperation, we propose a semantic-aware task clustering method for CMT-SemCom. We have formulated a sequential multi-stage optimization problem in which semantically aligned tasks are clustered once after a short initial training phase, and then end-to-end (E2E) joint training is conducted exclusively within the discovered groups. Specifically, the problem decomposes into two stages: (i) a semantic clustering problem leveraging hierarchical density-based spatial clustering, and (ii) an intra-cluster E2E CMT-SemCom learning problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates destructive cooperation and negative transfer, yielding accuracy gains compared to unclustered multi-tasking and individual training baselines.

</details>


### [301] [Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21475)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明确定性KV缓存驱逐无法估计注意力误差，而随机驱逐（泊松采样尾部+Hájek校正）可生成每步误差证书（覆盖率97%）。实验发现：问题感知驱逐在25-50%预算下几乎无成本；输出对数概率比证书更能预测失败；证书驱动的预算扩展无效。但证书在归因方面表现优异（AUC 0.73-0.75），可区分缓存引发和固有失败，并比随机或置信门控更好地调度重计算。随机化带来的是归因而非预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性KV缓存驱逐方法无法估计被丢弃token对注意力输出的影响，即存在不可知性（unknowability）问题，导致服务时无法可靠检测错误。

Method: 采用泊松采样尾部（Poisson-sampled tail）实现随机驱逐，利用已知包含概率通过Hájek校正修正softmax中的logit偏移，并使用抽样调查方差估计器作为每步误差证书。

Result: 误差证书经验覆盖率达97%且无精度损失；证书在区分缓存引发失败与固有失败时AUC达0.73-0.75，优于输出置信度（0.47-0.54）。预注册的七个断言中三个未通过：问题感知驱逐在25-50%预算下几乎无成本，输出对数概率比证书更能预测失败，证书驱动的预算扩展无效。

Conclusion: 随机化KV缓存驱逐带来的核心价值是归因能力（attribution）而非预测能力，可用于调度重计算和诊断失败来源。

Abstract: Deterministic KV-cache eviction keeps the top-$k$ tokens under an importance score and deletes the rest. We prove that this design cannot know what it destroyed: evicted values can be altered so that everything the serving system retains is unchanged while the true attention-output error grows arbitrarily, so no serving-time estimator of that error is consistent. Randomized eviction restores identifiability. With a Poisson-sampled tail at known inclusion probabilities, one logit offset performs the Hájek correction inside the softmax, and a survey-sampling variance estimator over the retained set becomes a per-step error certificate with 0.97 empirical coverage at no accuracy cost. On real workloads we pre-registered seven claims and lost three: question-aware eviction at 25--50\% budgets is nearly free; output log-probability predicts failure better than the certificate; certificate-gated budget escalation adds nothing. What survives is attribution: the certificate separates cache-induced from inherent failures (AUC 0.73--0.75, against 0.47--0.54 for output confidence) and schedules recomputation better than random or confidence gating. Randomization buys attribution, not prediction.

</details>


### [302] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于局部上下文加权的离散流匹配方法，通过改进连续时间马尔可夫链的采样过程和重新加权训练损失，显著提升生成质量并降低困惑度，在OpenWebText上生成困惑度降低63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散流匹配的因子化训练目标会使模型同时面对条件良好、可预测的token和高熵不确定的token，导致训练信号质量不均。作者发现每个token的不确定性与其局部上下文的密度密切相关，由此出发探索利用局部上下文信息改进离散生成模型。

Method: 1) 提出上下文加权采样器（context-weighted sampler），通过修改底层连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）融入局部上下文信息，几乎不增加计算开销；2) 提出缩放交叉熵损失函数（scaled cross-entropy loss），对不同token的训练信号进行重新加权，使模型更关注高不确定性token。

Result: 在OpenWebText上生成困惑度降低63%；在生成质量上与强半自回归块扩散基线相当，同时保持任意顺序生成的能力。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模中的重要因素，简单的上下文感知改进可以显著提升采样效率和训练效率。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [303] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KroQuant提出了一种基于Kronecker结构化可逆变换的后训练量化方法，用于扩散Transformer的W4A4量化。该方法对每32元素激活块应用变换，参数量少于per-channel scaling，计算速度比SmoothQuant快14%，并在PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell等模型上达到优于SVDQuant和LoRaQ的量化质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在W4A4量化下输出质量严重下降，因为激活值中的异常值无法被4位格式有效表示。现有方法在量化精度与推理代价之间存在权衡：SmoothQuant计算廉价但量化精度不足；固定Hadamard变换精度较好但在线计算成本高；学习型全d可逆变换校准效果最佳但每层每步需密集矩阵乘法，代价过高。

Method: KroQuant的核心是学习一个Kronecker结构化的可逆变换，应用于激活的每32元素块上。该块局部结构可通过小张量核心GEMM高效执行。离线LoRaQ权重校准用于吸收残余的逐权重量化误差。

Result: 在MI350 GPU上，KroQuant量化器内核比SmoothQuant内核快14%。在PixArt-Σ、SANA、FLUX.1-schnell等模型的W4A4 (MXFP4e2)设定下，KroQuant在MJHQ-30K和SDCI数据集上生成的输出比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更接近FP参考，同时保持或提升图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant通过Kronecker结构化可逆变换实现了在低推理成本下的高精度W4A4量化，参数量少于per-channel scaling，同时取得优于现有方法的量化质量。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [304] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTEL是一种通过令牌级错误定位来优化推理时计算效率的算法，利用环境反馈识别错误步骤、截断轨迹并分支生成新内容，在多个基准测试中显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展方法（如独立采样和多轮精炼）缺乏令牌级信用分配，导致有效推理前缀被频繁丢弃，计算效率低下。

Method: TTEL利用固定或环境反馈进行令牌级错误定位，通过比较有信息反馈下的条件概率与无上下文基线，隔离错误发生的步骤，然后截断轨迹并分支生成新内容，最大化重用有效前缀。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B的LiveCodeBench上，TTEL的pass@64达到71.0%，生成约一半的令牌数（360.4k vs 735.0k）；在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025数学基准上，TTEL在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507上均优于其他测试时基线方法。

Conclusion: TTEL在顺序推理领域建立了严格支配的帕累托前沿，证明了令牌级错误定位能显著提升推理时计算效率。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [305] [Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21480)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用有限样本有效性的审计标签来认证高召回率流水线中遗漏的相关项数量。核心发现：仅用候选集内部标签无法认证遗漏量的非平凡界，必须抽样被排除的池子。证明了零遗漏场景下排除池审计的标签复杂度下界（约N₀/m），并开发了一套基于二项和超几何反演的精确实证工具包，支持遗漏量认证、召回率转换、嵌套候选生成器族认证及压力测试证书生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高召回率流水线中，初始阶段遗漏的相关项会被后续所有阶段丢失，因此需要认证遗漏的相关项质量很小。现有方法缺乏有限样本有效性下的严格理论保证，且不清楚仅靠候选集内部标签能否完成这一认证。

Method: 理论分析：证明候选集内部标签无法认证非平凡遗漏界，必须抽样排除池；推导有限语料库下的匹配下界（N₀/m）。实践工具：基于二项和超几何反演而非渐近近似的精确实证方法，支持两池设计、嵌套族认证和压力测试。

Result: 证明了排除池审计在零遗漏场景下是最小极大速率最优的；开发了完整的有限样本有效认证工具包，可同时认证遗漏量、召回率、嵌套候选生成器族，并生成压力测试证书。

Conclusion: 任何有效的审计都必须抽样排除池，且排除池审计在标签复杂度上达到了理论最优。所提工具包可在固定候选生成器和审计规则的前提下，为高召回率流水线提供严格的有限样本有效性保证。

Abstract: An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. We first show that no procedure using only labels from inside the candidate set can certify any non-trivial bound on the missed mass: the audit must sample the excluded pool, the only region where unrecovered relevant items can lie. We then prove a matching finite-corpus lower bound. Any valid audit that certifies fewer than $m$ missed relevant items with high probability when none are present, even if adaptive and permitted to label the entire included pool, must inspect on the order of $N_0/m$ excluded-pool labels. Excluded-pool auditing is therefore minimax rate-optimal, not merely convenient, for missed-mass certification in the zero-miss regime. Building on this characterisation, we develop an exact finite-sample toolkit, using binomial and hypergeometric inversion rather than asymptotic approximation, that certifies missed mass, converts it to recall through a two-pool design, certifies pre-specified families of nested candidate generators simultaneously, and produces stress-test certificates against declared perturbation mechanisms. These certificates can be paired with observable review burden to select the least burdensome pre-specified candidate generator meeting a missed-mass target. Every guarantee holds under one discipline: the candidate generator, or the pre-specified family from which it is selected, and the audit rule are fixed before the certification labels are examined.

</details>


### [306] [Compact Latent Coordination for Autonomous Vehicles at Unsignalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21488)
*Gil Lifshits,Igal Bilik,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MAPS的分层深度强化学习架构，用于无信号交叉口的自动驾驶车辆协调。该架构包含一个中央Master Agent生成全局协调策略的连续嵌入（proto-plan），以及多个分散的Worker Agent将其与局部观测结合执行车辆控制。在72种交叉口配置中，MAPS实现了无碰撞导航并显著降低平均行驶时间，且从三智能体场景零样本迁移到五智能体场景时仍保持94%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL系统在无信号交叉口协调自动驾驶车辆时面临组合动作空间、依赖特权信息或智能体设计僵化等挑战。

Method: 提出Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS)分层DRL架构：中央Master Agent生成连续嵌入（proto-plan）编码全局协调策略，分散Worker Agent结合该嵌入与局部观测执行车辆控制，将策略意图与战术执行解耦。

Result: 在72种交叉口配置中实现无碰撞导航；显著降低平均行驶时间，优于SOTA基线；三智能体训练模型零样本迁移至五智能体场景时成功率达94%。

Conclusion: 基于proto-plan的分层学习为多车辆协调提供了一种有前景的框架，能够有效解耦全局策略与局部执行，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Coordinating autonomous vehicles at unsignalized intersections remains a critical challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems, which typically struggle with combinatorial action spaces, reliance on privileged information, or rigid agent designs. We propose Master-Agent Proto-plan System (MAPS), a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) architecture in which a centralized Master agent generates a compact, continuous embedding, denoted as proto-plan, that encodes a global coordination strategy. Decentralized Worker agents integrate this embedding with local observations to execute vehicle-specific control, decoupling strategic intent from tactical execution and enabling independent optimization of each module.
  As a proof-of-concept evaluation of this coordination mechanism, we test MAPS across 72 intersection configurations in HighwayEnv. MAPS achieves collision-free navigation while significantly reducing average travel time, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. The learned proto-plans further exhibit robust generalization: a system trained with three agents achieves a 94% success rate when deployed zero-shot to five-agent scenarios, confirming that proto-plan-based hierarchical learning provides a promising framework for multi-vehicle coordination.

</details>


### [307] [Zero-Flow Two-Sample Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21542)
*Yakun Wang,Leyang Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于零流准则的统计差异度量的双样本检验方法ZFD，并设计了实际检验流程ZF2ST，通过神经网络学习局部错位方向模式来检测分布差异，在保持统计校准的同时实现了强检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的双样本检验方法在高维复杂数据（如图像）上难以有效检测结构化分布变化，且存在灵活性与统计校准之间的权衡。

Method: 基于零流准则定义零流差异（ZFD）作为统计度量；将见证函数学习与假设评估分离，使ZF2ST能够灵活使用神经网络；分别开发了基于回归和基于功效最大化的见证函数学习方法。

Result: 在合成数据集和图像数据集上的实验表明，ZF2ST在检测结构化分布变化方面具有强检验功效，同时保持了良好校准的I型错误率。

Conclusion: ZF2ST通过分离见证学习与假设评估，有效结合了神经网络的灵活性与统计检验的严谨性，为高维复杂数据的双样本检验提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose a new approach to two-sample testing for deciding whether two sets of samples are drawn from the same distribution. The test is built on a statistical discrepancy based on the zero-flow criterion, termed zero-flow discrepancy (ZFD). We prove the validity of ZFD and propose a practical testing procedure, termed the zero-flow two-sample test (ZF2ST). The key idea is to learn how samples from the two distributions are locally misaligned and use the resulting directional pattern as evidence of distributional difference. By separating witness learning from hypothesis evaluation, ZF2ST can use flexible neural networks while maintaining valid statistical calibration. We develop both regression-based and power-maximized approaches for learning the witness. Experiments on synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that ZF2ST can achieve strong testing power for structured distributional changes while maintaining well-calibrated type-I error.

</details>


### [308] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X$^3$-OPD框架，通过跨模态同策略蒸馏，将文本大模型的推理能力迁移到音频-语言模型中，解决音频推理数据稀缺问题。方法包含三个层次对称语料库（文本推理语音化、音频事件推理、口语对话推理），实验表明在多个基准上显著提升音频推理和思维链质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频-语言模型在听觉感知上取得进展，但深度逻辑推理能力落后于纯文本大模型，主要原因是高质量音频推理数据的稀缺。

Method: 提出X$^3$-OPD跨模态同策略蒸馏框架：(1) 学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹；(2) 教师模型利用匹配文本输入和验证答案提供token级指导；(3) 构建三层对称语料库：文本推理→语音、音频事件推理（复杂声学场景）、口语对话推理（副语言线索）。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio和MMAR基准上，X$^3$-OPD显著提升了音频推理和思维链质量，同时在域偏移情况下较好地保留了模型已有能力。

Conclusion: 跨模态同策略蒸馏能有效将文本推理能力迁移至音频-语言模型，且三层语料库设计使得蒸馏范围扩展到非语言事件、韵律和对话上下文等不可文本恢复的推理内容。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [309] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EnsembleEGNN，一种分子构象集成基础模型，通过共享EGNN编码每个构象、Set Attention模块池化得到单个热力学感知的表示。在CREMP-CycPeptMPDB数据集上，从头训练失败(R²=0.005)，预训练后达到R²=0.477和Pearson r=0.699，优于序列BERT基线；与BERT联合训练后进一步提升至R²=0.538和Pearson r=0.737。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测通常使用单一代表性构象，但许多分子在溶液中以构象集合形式存在，忽视了构象系综提供的热力学信息，导致预测不够准确。

Method: 先通过共享EGNN层独立编码每个构象，再用Set Attention Block将所有构象表示池化为单个集成嵌入；预训练采用掩码token恢复、噪声坐标重建、成对距离重建的多任务自监督目标。

Result: 从头训练R²=0.005（完全失败）；预训练后R²=0.477, Pearson r=0.699；与BERT联合训练后R²=0.538, Pearson r=0.737，均优于序列BERT基线(R²=0.439, Pearson r=0.667)。

Conclusion: 将构象系综编码为单个热力学信息嵌入能显著改善环肽性质预测性能，尤其是结合序列信息时效果更佳。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [310] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimePNS是一个必要性感知的时间序列解释框架，通过反事实干预识别决策关键子序列，在充分性和必要性之间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于充分性的解释方法会赋予非必要但支持预测的虚假子序列高重要性，缺乏对必要性的考量。

Method: 两阶段设计：Stage I学习可识别的因果生成过程及充分性解释掩码；Stage II通过反事实干预获取必要性信号，监督时序门控机制精炼初始解释。

Result: 在合成和真实世界时间序列基准上，TimePNS能更准确识别决策关键子序列，并在充分性-必要性权衡上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种融合Pearl反事实必要性概念的时间序列解释框架，有效抑制了非必要成分并强调反事实必要的时序因素。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [311] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出扩展生成流（EFlows）及扩展流映射（EFMs），一种能够在连续和离散空间中实现可变尺寸/长度生成的生成式框架，通过沿扩展插值路径增广状态维度，并分解为扩展算子和传输映射两个可学习操作，将固定画布的流模型推广至输出尺寸作为自由度的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流的生成模型在参数化上受限于固定维度或固定序列长度，无法处理输出尺寸本身需要被学习和控制的任务（如图生成、变长序列生成）。

Method: 1. 定义EFlows：在分布之间沿扩展插值路径（expanding interpolant）流动，通过条件噪声增广状态维度。2. 提出EFMs：将任意两个时间步之间的映射分解为扩展算子（expand operator，基于当前状态增广新坐标/令牌）和传输映射（transport map，沿插值路径推动扩展状态）两个可学习操作。3. 扩展到离散单纯形，支持变尺寸图生成和变长序列生成。固定画布的流模型是该框架中扩展算子为单位映射的特例。

Result: 在连续和离散模态上，EFlows和EFMs被建立为一个原则性框架，适用于输出尺寸本身是学习得到的、可控的自由度的场景。

Conclusion: EFlows和EFMs为可变输出尺寸的生成任务提供了统一且原理性的方法，将固定尺寸流模型作为特例包含其中。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [312] [Sender and Receiver Energy Consumption in a Sensor Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21095)
*J M Fourneau,F. Quessette*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种包含能量包和数据包的新型模型，数据包传输需要发送方和接收方都有能量。能量包代表传输数据包所需的焦耳量子。在经典假设下，该复杂多队列同步系统的马尔可夫链稳态分布具有乘积形式解，并给出了遍历性充分条件及计算流量的数值算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络模型仅考虑数据包传输，忽略了收发双方均需消耗能量的现实约束。本文旨在建立一种同时考虑能量包和数据包的双端能量约束模型，以更真实地描述能量受限通信网络的行为。

Method: 构建能量包与数据包的双队列模型，其中能量包队列模拟电池，数据包发送需要发送方和接收方均有足够能量。采用马尔可夫链建模，通过乘积形式解分析稳态分布，并推导遍历性条件，设计数值算法计算流量。

Result: 在经典假设下，所提出的复杂多队列同步系统的马尔可夫链稳态分布具有乘积形式解；给出了系统遍历性的充分条件；证明了所提数值算法的收敛性和正确性。

Conclusion: 尽管能量包与数据包的双端同步机制增加了系统复杂度，但在一定条件下该系统仍具有良好的可解性（乘积形式解），并且可以通过数值算法有效求解流量值。

Abstract: We consider a new type of model with energy packets and data packets where the transmission of a data packet requires energy on both the sender and the receiver nodes. Energy packets is a discrete number of Joules representing the quantum of energy needed to send and receive a data packet. Both types of packets are stored in queues. The energy packet queue models a battery. Without energy on the sender, the emission is delayed until energy is available. When a sent packet arrives on a receiver which does not have enough energy, it is lost. This mechanism implies a new complex synchronization between several queues. Despite this complexity, we prove that under some classical assumptions the steady-state distribution of the Markov chain has a product form solution. We state sufficient conditions for ergodicity and we also prove the convergence and the correctness of a numerical algorithm to compute the values of the flows.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [313] [PortLBM: A Portable Lattice Boltzmann Tool Leveraging SYCL on AMD, NVIDIA, and Intel GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20650)
*Alexander Strack,Marcel Graf,Alexander Van Craen,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PortLBM是一个基于SYCL的可移植LBM框架，支持跨平台GPU和实时可视化。在NVIDIA、AMD和Intel GPU上的评估表明：stream布局在NVIDIA和Intel GPU上最优，bundle布局在AMD GPU最优；双网格方案吞吐量更高，单网格方案节省内存。没有一种配置在所有GPU上均最优，需针对系统进行调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着摩尔定律放缓，高性能计算转向GPU加速器，需要一种既保证可移植性又兼顾效率的编程模型来应对日益异构化的计算环境。

Method: 基于SYCL构建可移植LBM框架PortLBM，支持三种数据布局（stream、bundle、collision）和四种算法变体，在NVIDIA、AMD、Intel当代GPU上评估仿真吞吐量。

Result: 无单一配置在所有GPU上均达最优性能：stream布局在NVIDIA和Intel GPU上表现最佳，bundle布局在AMD GPU上因改善缓存效率而最优；双网格方案吞吐量更高，单网格方案适用于内存受限场景。

Conclusion: 在异构计算环境中，LBM软件需要具备可适应性和可移植性，系统特定的调优必不可少。

Abstract: The lattice Boltzmann method (LBM) is a well-established approach for simulating fluid flows at the mesoscopic scale. With the stagnation of Moore's law, high-performance computing has shifted toward GPU accelerators, necessitating programming models that ensure both portability and efficiency across diverse hardware platforms.
  We present PortLBM, an extensible portable LBM framework built on SYCL that integrates cross-platform GPU support with interactive real-time visualization. PortLBM supports diverse simulation scenarios ranging from Kármán vortex streets and wing flows to porous media, and is designed for easy extension with new algorithms and backends. As part of a performance portability study, we evaluate PortLBM on contemporary GPU architectures from NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel, examining the impact of three data layouts (stream, bundle, and collision) and four algorithmic variants on simulation throughput.
  Our results show that no single configuration achieves optimal performance across all GPU vendors, confirming the need for system-specific tuning. The stream layout maximizes bandwidth and performs best on the contemporary NVIDIA and Intel GPUs, while the bundle layout improves cache efficiency and excels on the AMD GPU. Two-lattice schemes achieve higher throughput while one-lattice schemes are preferable under memory constraints. Our work underscores the necessity for adaptable, portable LBM software in increasingly heterogeneous computing environments.

</details>


### [314] [A Framework for Reputation Aware Uninorm-driven Consensus Algorithms for Blockchain Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20700)
*Bruno Ramos-Cruz,Javier Andreu-Perez,David Richerby,Luis Martínez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于直觉模糊集（IFSs）和一致模聚合算子（UAOs）的声誉共识算法，解决现有共识机制中计算开销大、参与排他和权力中心化问题。该方法通过IFS表达声誉的不确定性，利用UAO动态监控声誉变化并强化正负声誉反馈，使验证者可修正过往失败。算法保持线性计算复杂度，不增加额外通信开销，实验表明在公平性和包容性上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识机制（如PoW、PoS）存在计算开销大、权益集中导致权力中心化和参与者排斥等问题。声誉共识算法虽然是一种替代方案，但声誉值本身具有不确定性，缺乏有效的建模与动态更新机制。

Method: 使用直觉模糊集（IFSs）对验证者的声誉进行建模，以表达声誉值中的不确定性和缺乏精确知识的状态；采用一致模聚合算子（UAOs）对声誉进行时序聚合监控，强化正（积极）声誉和负（消极）声誉的重要性，使得验证者可以弥补过往失败行为。

Result: 算法保持线性计算复杂度（O(n)），不增加底层共识协议之外的通信开销。实验结果表明该方法在性能评估上表现更优，提升了区块链网络的公平性和包容性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于IFS和UAO的声誉共识算法能够有效缓解传统共识机制中的中心化和排他问题，促进更公平和包容的区块链网络设计。

Abstract: The operation of blockchain is governed by consensus algorithms (CA). Several consensus mechanisms require significant computational power, while others necessitate high amounts of stakes to select the participant to validate and verify the transactions in the block, leading to centralisation of power and participant exclusion. This paper proposes a novel methodology to address these issues in reputation-based consensus algorithms by studying the reputation behaviour of the validator using intuitionistic fuzzy sets (IFSs) and uninorm aggregation operations (UAOs). Our approach uses IFSs to express the "reputation" because the reputation values in a consensus algorithm eventually imply uncertainty, and IFSs facilitate the representation of a lack of precise knowledge about reputation. Moreover, this methodology utilises uninorm aggregation operations to monitor reputation over time and reinforces the importance of negative and positive reputation. Consequently, this solution allows validators to rectify past failures in subsequent verification processes and foster an equitable consensus algorithm design. The proposed framework maintains linear computational complexity and does not introduce additional communication overhead beyond the underlying consensus protocol. Supported by experimental results, our methodology demonstrates improved performance and evaluation, promising advancements in blockchain network fairness and inclusivity.

</details>


### [315] [The Consensus Number of Untraceable Cryptocurrencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20929)
*Christian Cachin,David Lehnherr,Juan Villacis,François-Xavier Wicht*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种加密货币发送者不可追踪性设计方案：经典线性方案（LUAT）保存整个掩码集并追加空值，导致账本随交易增长；常量状态方案（CUAT）消耗并替换整个集合。研究发现两者在同步性上的代价不同——LUAT牺牲存储，CUAT牺牲同步和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究发送者不可追踪性中两种设计（保留掩码集 vs. 消耗替换掩码集）对同步性（共识复杂度、无饥饿性）的影响，揭示隐私与性能之间的权衡。

Method: 形式化定义线性不可追踪资产转移对象（LUAT）和常量不可追踪资产转移对象（CUAT），将其定位到共识层次中。通过冲突图分析CUAT中掩码集相交时的并发限制，并区分弱不可追踪性和强不可追踪性两种隐私模型。

Result: LUAT的共识数为2（与掩码集大小和不可追踪性概念无关），且具有无饥饿性；CUAT在弱不可追踪性下共识数无界，在强不可追踪性下共识数随掩码集大小二次增长，且不保证无饥饿性。

Conclusion: 两种方案为相同的隐私付出了不同的代价：LUAT支付存储代价，CUAT支付同步和公平性代价。

Abstract: Sender untraceability hides the account spent by a cryptocurrency transfer among a set of candidates, its masking set. What a transfer does to that set separates two designs: classical schemes retain the whole set and append a nullifier marking the spent account, so the ledger grows with every transfer; constant-state schemes instead consume and replace the entire set. We ask how this choice affects synchronization.
  We formalize the two designs as the linear and constant untraceable asset transfer objects (LUAT and CUAT) and locate them in the consensus hierarchy. In LUAT, transfers from distinct accounts commute. Its consensus number is 2, compared with 1 for standard asset transfer, independently of the masking-set size and of the untraceability notion, and LUAT is starvation-free. Partitioning the accounts into fixed masking sets lets exhausted sets be garbage-collected without increasing that number.
  In CUAT, a transfer consumes and replaces every account of its masking set, so two transfers whose sets intersect cannot both take effect. We formalize this with the conflict graph on masking sets, whose edges join sets sharing an account. Under weak untraceability, which protects a transaction in isolation, the consensus number is unbounded already for one-round protocols. Under strong untraceability, which protects against an observer of the complete history, untraceability holds on a history exactly when any two accounts sharing a masking set occur in the same number of the masking sets in it. This uniform incidence bounds the conflict graph, and matching constructions attain it, so the consensus number is determined exactly and grows quadratically in the masking-set size. Finally, CUAT is not starvation-free. The two objects therefore pay for the same privacy differently: LUAT in storage, CUAT in synchronization and fairness.

</details>


### [316] [Multimmit: Extending Blocks for Faster Finality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21021)
*Andrew Lewis-Pye,Patrick O'Grady*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Multimmit协议通过结合共识层与多链数据分发，在n≥5f+1的处理器上实现高效的状态机复制，交易区块在分发后预期3δ内完成排序，故障下性能优雅降级，且共识流量与交易量无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代区块链系统中，现有SMR协议在区块排序时面临两难：认证方式（certified）鲁棒但增加消息延迟，非认证方式（uncertified）快速但数据获取时性能急剧下降。Raptr虽采取折中方案，但对顺序敏感——单个恶意生产者扣留数据即可使系统无法进入乐观路径。

Method: Multimmit针对n≥5f+1个处理器，将每视图一轮投票的共识层与多链数据分发相结合。投票时，各节点针对领导者的提案，按链报告其可支持的程度，并可将新区块附加于投票中。领导者无法在最终确定自己区块的同时排除诚实链上已良好传播的新区块。

Result: 交易区块在分发后预期3δ内完成排序，最优可达2δ。故障下性能优雅降级：单个故障生产者仅延迟自己链上的区块，其他链最多等待一个视图。共识流量为每视图数十KB，与交易量无关。

Conclusion: Multimmit解决了Raptr对顺序敏感的问题，提供了更优的延迟保证和故障下的优雅降级，同时保持极低的共识开销。

Abstract: To meet the throughput demands of modern blockchain systems, protocols for State Machine Replication (SMR) increasingly have many processors disseminate blocks of transactions in parallel, with consensus then establishing a total ordering on the blocks of all producers. Such designs face a choice as to when a block may enter the ordering. Certified approaches wait for a quorum to attest a block's availability, which is robust but adds message delays to every transaction. Uncertified approaches let proposals reference blocks immediately, which is fast but degrades rapidly when referenced data must be fetched on the critical path. Raptr, the state of the art, takes a middle course, finalising the longest prefix of the leader's proposal that a quorum holds, so that no processor ever blocks or fetches. The remaining weakness is sensitivity to order: if the data behind a single early batch is withheld, the proposal finalises little or nothing, so individual faulty producers can still deny the system its optimistic path.
  We present Multimmit, a protocol for $n \ge 5f+1$ processors combining a consensus layer requiring one round of voting per view with multi-chain data dissemination. Votes are cast relative to the leader's proposal, reporting per chain how far the voter can support it, and may themselves attest fresh blocks beyond it. A transaction block disseminated at time $t$ is ordered by $t+3δ$ in expectation and $t+2δ$ at best, measured from the block's dissemination rather than the leader's proposal. Degradation under faults is graceful: a faulty producer delays only its own chain's blocks, costing other chains at most a one-view wait for placement. No leader can both finalise its leader block and exclude a fresh, well-circulated block of an honest chain. Consensus traffic is tens of kilobytes per view, independent of transaction volume.

</details>


### [317] [Solving Large Traveling Salesman Problems (TSPs) by a Recursive Clustering Algorithm and a Scalable FPGA-Based Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21182)
*Junqi Huang,Hawraa Abbas Almurieb,T. Nandha Kumar,Haider A. F. Almurib,Fabrizio Lombardi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种递归聚类方法解决旅行商问题（TSP），将城市划分成最多包含5个城市的簇，结合约束TSP和模拟退火进行簇内路由，并在FPGA上实现。该方法通过使用簇质心作为路由的进出点，利用FPGA内存存储簇信息，实现了更好的可扩展性和更高的运行频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSP在众多应用中具有关键作用，持续吸引大量研究关注，现有方法在处理大量城市时面临可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 采用递归聚类将城市划分为最多5个城市的簇，每个簇有其质心；使用约束TSP和模拟退火进行簇内路由，以相邻簇质心作为进出点；簇间连接通过模拟退火建立；在FPGA上实现，距离计算采用平方欧氏距离以降低资源消耗。

Result: FPGA实现证明该方法可路由大量城市，可扩展性仅取决于FPGA内存容量而非逻辑硬件；与先前方法相比，能处理更多城市，具有更好的可扩展性和更高的运行频率。

Conclusion: 该方法通过聚类降低问题规模，结合FPGA内存优势，突破了传统逻辑硬件的限制，为大规模TSP问题提供了一种高效可扩展的硬件实现方案。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) continues to attract significant research interest due to its critical role in various applications. This paper introduces a recursive clustering approach that divides cities into a limited number of clusters, each containing up to five cities and its own centroid. Constrained TSP and simulated annealing techniques are employed to route cities within each cluster, using the centroids of neighboring clusters as entry and exit points for the routing process. This method offers the benefit of producing accurate and cost-effective routing solutions, due to the reduced number of cities in each cluster. The connections between cluster centroids are established using simulated annealing. The FPGA-based proposed hardware implementation demonstrates the feasibility of routing a large number of cities, as the approach leverages memory to store cluster information. Consequently, the method is independent of the FPGA's logic hardware, and its scalability depends on the FPGA's memory capacity. Furthermore, distance calculations using approximate methods for the FPGA execution are compared and the squared Euclidean is chosen due to its low resources' utilization. Compared to previous methods, the proposed approach can handle more cities for routing, offering better scalability and a higher operating frequency.

</details>


### [318] [Controlled Periodic Synchronization for Efficient Data-Parallel Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21224)
*Imane Ettifouri,Mostapha Zbakh,Claude Tadonki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出受控周期数据并行（CPDP）策略，通过交替本地更新与梯度AllReduce+SlowMo参数平均的协调步骤，在通信受限的分布式训练中将同步频率作为系统参数进行调优。在Grid'5000广域网部署下，K=4时CPDP相比标准DDP提升2.44个百分点的峰值准确率，同时平均wall-clock时间降低13.8%，表明同步频率是通信约束下分布式训练的实用控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DDP每轮迭代都同步梯度，在快速局域网有效，但在地理分布式环境中对通信延迟和网络波动敏感。现有周期方法如LocalSGD降低同步频率但主要依赖参数平均，当工作节点轨迹发散时效果不足。因此需要探索同步频率作为系统参数来优化通信受限分布式训练的性能。

Method: 提出CPDP（Controlled Periodic Data Parallelism）策略，兼容PyTorch-DDP。核心思想：交替进行K步本地更新与一次协调步骤，协调步骤同时执行梯度AllReduce和SlowMo参数平均。通过调整K值控制同步频率，在通信开销和模型质量之间取得平衡。

Result: 在ResNet-50/CIFAR-100固定学习率0.1设置下：K=2时CPDP比DDP提升2.28个百分点但增加wall-clock时间；K=4时提升2.44个百分点且平均wall-clock时间降低13.8%。直接分析显示K=4时暴露的同步时间约为DDP的一半。在ViT-S/CIFAR-100和ResNet-50/TinyImageNet上CPDP保持竞争力且普遍优于LocalSGD。

Conclusion: 同步频率可作为通信受限分布式训练中的实用控制参数，CPDP通过合理选择同步周期（如K=4）能够在广域网环境下实现比标准DDP更好的准确率-时间权衡。

Abstract: Data-parallel training relies on frequent gradient synchronization across workers. Standard DDP synchronizes gradients at every iteration, which is effective on fast local-area networks but increasingly sensitive to communication latency and network variability in geographically distributed environments. Periodic methods such as LocalSGD reduce synchronization frequency but rely mainly on parameter averaging, which may be insufficient when worker trajectories diverge. This paper studies synchronization frequency as a systems parameter for communication-constrained distributed training. We evaluate Controlled Periodic Data Parallelism (CPDP), a PyTorch-DDP-compatible strategy that alternates local updates with a reconciliation step combining gradient AllReduce and SlowMo parameter averaging. Experiments are conducted on Grid'5000 across intra-site clusters and a cross-site WAN deployment spanning Nancy and Sophia with 16.6 ms RTT. In the main fixed learning-rate setting of 0.1 on ResNet-50/CIFAR-100, CPDP achieves the highest peak test accuracy among the evaluated configurations. At K=2, CPDP improves over DDP by 2.28 percentage points but incurs additional wall-clock time. At K=4, CPDP improves over DDP by 2.44 percentage points while reducing average wall-clock time by 13.8%. Direct profiling shows that exposed synchronization time at K=4 is roughly half that of DDP, explaining the improved WAN accuracy-time trade-off. Additional experiments on ViT-S/CIFAR-100 and ResNet-50/TinyImageNet show that CPDP remains competitive with DDP and generally improves over LocalSGD. Overall, the results show that synchronization frequency is a practical control parameter for distributed training under communication constraints.

</details>

### [319] [Hardware-Software Co-Design for Float16 On-Device Training on RISC-V Single-Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21130)
*Benjamin Hubinet,Pierre-Alain Moellic,Olivier Savry,Olivier Potin,Jean-Baptiste Rigaud*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于RISC-V Zfh（标量float16）和Zvfh（向量float16）扩展的开源框架，在资源受限的单核RISC-V上实现完整的设备端训练，相比float32减少约50%内存占用且模型性能损失极小，并支持迁移学习中的层冻结。该框架基于AIfES搭建，通过FPGA验证Zfh仅增加1.15% LUT6和0.05% FF开销，同时讨论了Zvfh的实现架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的嵌入式设备上实现完整的设备端训练（而非仅推理）面临内存和计算资源的严重限制，现有方案通常依赖float32导致内存占用过高，缺乏对低精度浮点训练的原生硬件支持和开源框架。

Method: 利用RISC-V标准扩展Zfh（标量float16）和Zvfh（向量float16）实现低精度训练；基于AIfES开源框架（模块化、可扩展的DNN训练推理框架）构建；引入层冻结机制支持迁移学习与微调；在RV64GC超标量乱序FPGA软核上评估硬件开销。

Result: 相比float32，float16训练内存占用降低约50%，模型性能退化极小；Zfh扩展在FPGA上仅增加1.15% LUT6和0.05% FF逻辑开销，工作频率175MHz。

Conclusion: 基于标准RISC-V float16扩展的开源框架能有效支持资源受限设备上的完整训练，内存效率显著提升且硬件代价可忽略，向量扩展Zvfh的集成方案具有可行性。

Abstract: By leveraging standard RISC-V extensions, namely Zfh (scalar float16) and Zvfh (vector float16), this work proposes an open-source framework to enable complete on-device training on resource-constrained RISC-V single-core. Our approach allows memory footprint reduction by about 50% as compared to using float32 and with minimal model performance degradation. We also facilitate transfer learning and fine-tuning scenarios by incorporating layer-freezing capabilities. Our work builds onto AIfES, an open-source, modular and generic DNN training and inference framework for embedded systems that can be extended with custom hardware-specific functions. The benefits of float16 is further emphasized by outlining the low area overhead of Zfh on a RV64GC super-scalar out-of-order FPGA softcore (+1.15% LUT6 and +0.05% FF at 175MHz). Finally, we discuss the architecture of a Zvfh implementation within the same RISC-V core.

</details>


### [320] [Chip Floorplanning Combining Convex and Non-convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21408)
*Yilihamujiang Yimamu,Guillem Pastor-Rué,Jordi Cortadella*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合非凸与凸优化的多阶段固定轮廓布图规划框架，包含三个连续阶段：初始布局（二次优化生成拓扑感知的起始配置）、全局布图（Adam投影梯度法最小化线长和重叠）、合法化（对数变换模型消除残余重叠）。在MCNC、GSRC和HB+基准测试上，HPWL平均降低至少1%-5%，达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布图规划是VLSI物理设计的早期关键阶段，其质量直接影响互连线长、芯片性能和下游设计效率，现有方法在固定轮廓约束下难以同时实现高质量的线长优化和重叠消除。

Method: 采用三阶段框架：(1) 初始布局阶段：通过二次优化对模块连接性进行布局，生成拓扑感知的起始配置；(2) 全局布图阶段：使用基于Adam的投影梯度法求解非凸优化，最小化线长和重叠；(3) 合法化阶段：利用水平/垂直约束图构建对数变换模型，以凸优化形式消除残余重叠。

Result: 在MCNC、GSRC和HB+基准测试上，相比最先进的布图规划器，平均HPWL降低至少1%（部分基准）和5%（其他基准），达到了最先进的线长质量。

Conclusion: 该三阶段框架通过结合非凸和凸优化策略，有效解决了固定轮廓布图规划中线长优化与重叠消除的权衡问题，显著提升了线长质量，为VLSI物理设计提供了新的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Floorplanning is a critical early stage of VLSI physical design, and its quality directly impacts interconnect wirelength, chip performance, and downstream design efficiency. This article presents a multi-stage fixed-outline floorplanning framework that combines non-convex and convex optimization. The framework operates in three successive phases. In the initial floorplanning phase, a quadratic placement optimizes module connectivity to produce a topology-aware starting configuration with strong net clustering, albeit with significant module overlaps. In the global floorplanning phase, Adam-based projected gradient method is employed to solve non-convex optimization that minimizes wirelength and overlap. In the legalization phase, a log-transformed model exploiting horizontal and vertical constraint graphs eliminates residual overlaps under a convex formulation. Experiments on the MCNC, GSRC and HB+ benchmarks demonstrate that the framework achieves state-of-the-art wirelength quality, reducing average HPWL by at least 1\% and 5\% compared to state-of-the-art floorplanners on tested benchmarks.

</details>
